   74:         ####################################################
   75:         # 1. Content identity

   78:         content_id = str(
   79:             trend.get(
   80:                 "content_id",
   81:                 "",
   82:             )
   83:         ).strip()
   84: 
   85:         if not content_id:
   86:             raise RuntimeError(
   87:                 "Production topic has no content_id."
   88:             )
   89: 
   90:         ####################################################
   91:         # 2. Choose production format
   92:         ####################################################
   93: 
   94:         topic_title = str(
   95:             trend.get(
   96:                 "title",
   97:                 "",
   98:             )
   99:         ).strip()
  100: 
  101:         genre = str(
  102:             trend.get(
  103:                 "genre",
  104:                 "",
  105:             )
  106:         ).strip()
  107: 
  108:         format_decision = (

  110:                 topic=topic_title,
  111:                 genre=genre,
  112:                 authorized_movie_source=False,
  113:             )
  114:         )
  115: 
  116:         trend["content_format"] = {

  120:             "preferred_media": format_decision.preferred_media,
  121:             "narration_mode": format_decision.narration_mode,
  122:             "pacing": format_decision.pacing,
  123:             "rights_gate_required": format_decision.rights_gate_required,
  124:             "reference_profile": format_decision.reference_profile,
  125:         }
  126: 
  127:         ####################################################
  128:         # 3. Research topic
  129:         ####################################################
  130: 
  131:         if (
  132:             format_decision.format_name
  133:             == "famous_movie_commentary"
  134:         ):
  135: 
  136:             movie_title = (
  137:                 MovieTopicParser.extract_movie_title(
  138:                     topic_title
  139:                 )
  140:             )
  141: 
  142:             if not movie_title:
  143:                 raise RuntimeError(
  144:                     "Movie commentary topic has no recognized "
  145:                     f"movie title: {topic_title}"
  146:                 )
  147: 
  148:             trend["movie_title"] = movie_title
  149: 
  150:             knowledge_pack = await asyncio.to_thread(
  151:                     self.movie_research_service.research,
  152:                     movie_title=movie_title,
  153:                     commentary_topic=topic_title,
  154:                 )
  155: 
  156:             research_text = (
  157:                 self.movie_research_service.build_research_text(
  158:                     knowledge_pack
  159:                 )
  160:             )
  161: 
  162:         else:
  163: 
  164:             knowledge_pack = await asyncio.to_thread(
  165:                     self.research_service.research,
  166:                     topic_title,
  167:                 )
  168: 
  169:             research_text = (
  170:                 self.research_service.build_research_text(
  171:                     knowledge_pack
  172:                 )
  173:             )
  174: 
  175:         if not research_text.strip():
  176:             raise RuntimeError(
  177:                 "Research returned no usable grounded "
  178:                 f"information for: {topic_title}"
  179:             )
  180: 
  181:         trend["research"] = research_text
  182:         trend["knowledge"] = knowledge_pack.to_dict()
  183: 
  184:         ####################################################
  185:         # 4. Generate content
  186:         ####################################################
  187:         generated = await self.generator.generate(
  188:             trend
  189:         )
  190: 
  191:         if generated is None:
  192:             raise RuntimeError(
  193:                 "ContentGenerator returned None."
  194:             )
  195: 
  196:         ####################################################
  197:         # 3. Validate generated content
  198:         ####################################################
  199: 
  200:         validation = (
  201:             self.content_validator.validate(
  202:                 generated
  203:             )
  204:         )
  205: 
  206:         ####################################################
  207:         # 4. Repair rejected content
  208:         ####################################################
  209: 
  210:         if not validation["valid"]:
  211: 
  212:             repaired = await self.generator.repair(
  213:                 content=generated,
  214:                 trend=trend,
  215:                 issues=validation["issues"],
  216:             )
  217: 
  218:             if repaired is None:
  219:                 raise RuntimeError(
  220:                     "Generated content failed validation "
  221:                     "and automatic repair failed."
  222:                 )
  223: 
  224:             repaired_validation = (
  225:                 self.content_validator.validate(
  226:                     repaired
  227:                 )
  228:             )
  229: 
  230:             if not repaired_validation["valid"]:
  231:                 raise RuntimeError(
  232:                     "Repaired content still failed "
  233:                     "validation: "
  234:                     f"{repaired_validation['issues']}"
  235:                 )
  236: 
  237:             generated = repaired
  238:             validation = repaired_validation
  239: 
  240:         ####################################################
  241:         # 5. Build narration storyboard
  242:         ####################################################
  243: 
  244:         storyboard = self.storyboard.generate(
  245:             generated
  246:         )
  247: 
  248:         if not storyboard:
  249:             raise RuntimeError(
  250:                 "StoryboardGenerator produced no scenes."
  251:             )
  252: 
  253:         ####################################################
  254:         # 6. Acquire real licensed media
  255:         ####################################################
  256: 
  257:         media_groups = []
  258: 
  259:         topic = str(
  260:             trend.get(
  261:                 "title",
  262:                 "",
  263:             )
  264:         ).strip()
  265: 
  266:         genre = str(
  267:             trend.get(
  268:                 "genre",
  269:                 "",
  270:             )
  271:         ).strip()
  272: 
  273:         ####################################################
  274:         # Stage 2B - semantic visual micro-beats
  275:         #
  276:         # Narration remains four grounded story stages.
  277:         # Visuals are planned independently at a denser cadence.
  278:         ####################################################
  279: 
  280:         visual_beats = self.visual_beat_planner.plan(
  281:             content=generated,
  282:             topic=topic,
  283:             genre=genre,
  284:             format_name=(
  285:                 format_decision.format_name
  286:             ),
  287:         )
  288: 
  289:         if not visual_beats:
  290:             raise RuntimeError(
  291:                 "VisualBeatPlanner produced no visual beats."
  292:             )
  293: 
  294:         visual_beat_dicts = (
  295:             self.visual_beat_planner.to_dicts(
  296:                 visual_beats
  297:             )
  298:         )
  299: 
  300:         for index, scene in enumerate(
  301:             storyboard,
  302:             start=1,
  303:         ):
  304: 
  305:             queries = (
  306:                 self.media_query_builder.build(
  307:                     topic=topic,
  308:                     genre=genre,
  309:                     narration=scene.narration,
  310:                 )
  311:             )[:3]
  312: 
  313:             authorized_scene_assets = []
  314: 
  315:             for query in queries:
  316: 
  317:                 found = await (
  318:                     self.media_provider
  319:                     .search_and_download(
  320:                         query=query,
  321:                         content_id=content_id,
  322:                         limit=1,
  323:                     )
  324:                 )
  325: 
  326:                 if not found:
  327:                     continue
  328: 
  329:                 try:
  330:                     authorized = (
  331:                         self.asset_collector.authorize(
  332:                             found,
  333:                             content_id=content_id,
  334:                         )
  335:                     )
  336:                 except RuntimeError:
  337:                     continue
  338: 
  339:                 image_asset = next(
  340:                     (
  341:                         asset
  342:                         for asset in authorized
  343:                         if asset.asset_type
  344:                         == "image"
  345:                     ),
  346:                     None,
  347:                 )
  348: 
  349:                 if image_asset is None:
  350:                     continue
  351: 
  352:                 authorized_scene_assets.append(
  353:                     image_asset
  354:                 )
  355: 
  356:                 # Current renderer needs only one image
  357:                 # per narration scene. Stop immediately.
  358:                 break
  359: 
  360:             if not authorized_scene_assets:
  361:                 raise RuntimeError(
  362:                     "Scene "
  363:                     f"{index} produced no authorized "
  364:                     "image after limited media search. "
  365:                     f"Queries: {queries}"
  366:                 )
  367: 
  368:             media_groups.append(
  369:                 authorized_scene_assets
  370:             )
  371: 
  372:         if (
  373:             len(media_groups)
  374:             != len(storyboard)
  375:         ):
  376:             raise RuntimeError(
  377:                 "Storyboard/media group mismatch."
  378:             )
  379:         ####################################################
  380:         # Stage 2B - beat-specific authorized media
  381:         #
  382:         # IMPORTANT:
  383:         # This layer is deliberately fail-soft.
  384:         #
  385:         # Every beat has a known-good authorized fallback from
  386:         # its narration stage. A failed provider query therefore
  387:         # reduces visual specificity instead of failing the video.
  388:         ####################################################
  389: 
  390:         beat_render_assets = []
  391:         beat_media_evidence = []
  392: 
  393:         semantic_enrichment_attempts = 0
  394:         semantic_enrichment_successes = 0
  395:         semantic_fallback_count = 0
  396: 
  397:         for beat in visual_beats:
  398: 
  399:             narration_index = int(
  400:                 beat.narration_index
  401:             )
  402: 
  403:             stage_index = max(
  404:                 0,
  405:                 min(
  406:                     narration_index - 1,
  407:                     len(media_groups) - 1,
  408:                 ),
  409:             )
  410: 
  411:             fallback_asset = next(
  412:                 (
  413:                     asset
  414:                     for asset in media_groups[stage_index]
  415:                     if asset.asset_type == "image"
  416:                 ),
  417:                 None,
  418:             )
  419: 
  420:             if fallback_asset is None:
  421:                 raise RuntimeError(
  422:                     "Stage 2B fallback media missing for "
  423:                     f"narration stage {narration_index}."
  424:                 )
  425: 
  426:             selected_asset = fallback_asset
  427:             selected_via = "stage_fallback"
  428: 
  429:             query = str(
  430:                 beat.search_query
  431:             ).strip()
  432: 
  433:             if query and semantic_enrichment_attempts < self.MAX_SEMANTIC_MEDIA_ENRICHMENTS:
  434: 
  435:                 semantic_enrichment_attempts += 1
  436: 
  437:                 try:
  438: 
  439:                     found = await (
  440:                         self.media_provider
  441:                         .search_and_download(
  442:                             query=query,
  443:                             content_id=content_id,
  444:                             limit=1,
  445:                         )
  446:                     )
  447: 
  448:                     if found:
  449: 
  450:                         try:
  451: 
  452:                             authorized = (
  453:                                 self.asset_collector.authorize(
  454:                                     found,
  455:                                     content_id=content_id,
  456:                                 )
  457:                             )
  458: 
  459:                         except RuntimeError:
  460: 
  461:                             authorized = []
  462: 
  463:                         candidate = next(
  464:                             (
  465:                                 asset
  466:                                 for asset in authorized
  467:                                 if asset.asset_type == "image"
  468:                             ),
  469:                             None,
  470:                         )
  471: 
  472:                         if candidate is not None:
  473: 
  474:                             selected_asset = candidate
  475:                             selected_via = "semantic_query"
  476:                             semantic_enrichment_successes += 1
  477: 
  478:                 except Exception:
  479: 
  480:                     # Beat enrichment must never make a valid
  481:                     # production fail. The original authorized
  482:                     # scene asset remains the fallback.
  483:                     selected_asset = fallback_asset
  484:                     selected_via = "stage_fallback"
  485: 
  486:             if selected_via == "stage_fallback":
  487:                 semantic_fallback_count += 1
  488: 
  489:             beat_render_assets.append(
  490:                 selected_asset
  491:             )
  492: 
  493:             beat_media_evidence.append(
  494:                 {
  495:                     "beat_id": beat.beat_id,
  496:                     "narration_index": narration_index,
  497:                     "purpose": beat.purpose,
  498:                     "query": query,
  499:                     "selected_via": selected_via,
  500:                     "asset_id": selected_asset.asset_id,
  501:                     "source_name": selected_asset.source_name,
  502:                     "source_url": selected_asset.source_url,
  503:                     "license_name": selected_asset.license_name,
  504:                     "commercial_use_allowed": (
  505:                         selected_asset.commercial_use_allowed
  506:                     ),
  507:                 }
  508:             )
  509: 
  510:         if (
  511:             len(beat_render_assets)
  512:             != len(visual_beats)
  513:         ):
  514:             raise RuntimeError(
  515:                 "Visual beat/media timeline mismatch."
  516:             )
  517: 
  518:         ####################################################
  519:         # 7. Temporary renderer compatibility
  520:         #
  521:         # The existing renderer still accepts one image
  522:         # object per narration scene. For this integration
  523:         # test we pass the strongest authorized IMAGE from
  524:         # each group.
  525:         #
  526:         # The next renderer patch will consume the complete
  527:         # media_groups structure and produce multiple cuts.
  528:         ####################################################
  529: 
  530:         render_assets = []
  531: 
  532:         for index, group in enumerate(
  533:             media_groups,
  534:             start=1,
  535:         ):
  536: 
  537:             image_asset = next(
  538:                 (
  539:                     asset
  540:                     for asset in group
  541:                     if asset.asset_type
  542:                     == "image"
  543:                 ),
  544:                 None,
  545:             )
  546: 
  547:             if image_asset is None:
  548:                 raise RuntimeError(
  549:                     "Scene "
  550:                     f"{index} has no image-compatible "
  551:                     "asset for the current renderer."
  552:                 )
  553: 
  554:             render_assets.append(
  555:                 image_asset
  556:             )
  557: 
  558:         ####################################################
  559:         # 8. Build production package
  560:         ####################################################
  561: 
  562:         package = await self.package_builder.build(
  563:             content=generated,
  564:             scenes=storyboard,
  565:             images=render_assets,
  566:             trend=trend,
  567:         )
  568: 
  569:         ####################################################
  570:         # 9. Render
  571:         ####################################################
  572: 
  573:         # BLOCK5_VISUAL_QA_BEGIN
  574:         #
  575:         # The previous Stage2B implementation could legally
  576:         # resolve many semantic beats to the same authorized
  577:         # fallback image. That produced a technically valid
  578:         # 12-cut video which visually behaved like a slideshow.
  579:         #
  580:         # Rights authorization has already happened upstream.
  581:         # This gate does not authorize media and does not weaken
  582:         # any rights/evidence requirement. It only evaluates the
  583:         # final authorized beat sequence before expensive render.
  584: 
  585:         def _block5_asset_identity(asset):
  586:             if asset is None:
  587:                 return ""
  588: 
  589:             for attribute in (
  590:                 "image_path",
  591:                 "file_path",
  592:                 "local_path",
  593:                 "path",
  594:                 "source_url",
  595:                 "asset_id",
  596:             ):
  597:                 value = getattr(
  598:                     asset,
  599:                     attribute,
  600:                     None,
  601:                 )
  602: 
  603:                 if value:
  604:                     return str(value).strip().lower()
  605: 
  606:             if isinstance(asset, dict):
  607:                 for key in (
  608:                     "image_path",
  609:                     "file_path",
  610:                     "local_path",
  611:                     "path",
  612:                     "source_url",
  613:                     "asset_id",
  614:                 ):
  615:                     value = asset.get(key)
  616: 
  617:                     if value:
  618:                         return str(value).strip().lower()
  619: 
  620:             return str(asset).strip().lower()
  621: 
  622:         block5_identities = [
  623:             _block5_asset_identity(asset)
  624:             for asset in beat_render_assets
  625:         ]
  626: 
  627:         if (
  628:             not block5_identities
  629:             or len(block5_identities)
  630:             != len(visual_beat_dicts)
  631:         ):
  632:             raise RuntimeError(
  633:                 "Visual QA rejected semantic sequence: "
  634:                 "beat/image coverage mismatch."
  635:             )
  636: 
  637:         block5_counts = {}
  638: 
  639:         for identity in block5_identities:
  640:             if not identity:
  641:                 raise RuntimeError(
  642:                     "Visual QA rejected semantic sequence: "
  643:                     "unidentifiable authorized asset."
  644:                 )
  645: 
  646:             block5_counts[identity] = (
  647:                 block5_counts.get(identity, 0)
  648:                 + 1
  649:             )
  650: 
  651:         block5_distinct_assets = len(
  652:             block5_counts
  653:         )
  654: 
  655:         block5_largest_share = (
  656:             max(block5_counts.values())
  657:             / len(block5_identities)
  658:         )
  659: 
  660:         block5_max_repeat_run = 1
  661:         block5_current_repeat_run = 1
  662: 
  663:         for block5_index in range(
  664:             1,
  665:             len(block5_identities),
  666:         ):
  667:             if (
  668:                 block5_identities[block5_index]
  669:                 == block5_identities[
  670:                     block5_index - 1
  671:                 ]
  672:             ):
  673:                 block5_current_repeat_run += 1
  674:                 block5_max_repeat_run = max(
  675:                     block5_max_repeat_run,
  676:                     block5_current_repeat_run,
  677:                 )
  678:             else:
  679:                 block5_current_repeat_run = 1
  680: 
  681:         block5_minimum_distinct = min(
  682:             4,
  683:             len(block5_identities),
  684:         )
  685: 
  686:         if (
  687:             block5_distinct_assets
  688:             < block5_minimum_distinct
  689:         ):
  690:             raise RuntimeError(
  691:                 "Visual diversity gate rejected sequence: "
  692:                 f"{block5_distinct_assets} distinct assets "
  693:                 f"for {len(block5_identities)} beats; "
  694:                 f"minimum is {block5_minimum_distinct}."
  695:             )
  696: 
  697:         if block5_max_repeat_run > 2:
  698:             raise RuntimeError(
  699:                 "Visual diversity gate rejected sequence: "
  700:                 f"same asset appears for "
  701:                 f"{block5_max_repeat_run} consecutive beats; "
  702:                 "maximum is 2."
  703:             )
  704: 
  705:         if (
  706:             len(block5_identities) >= 8
  707:             and block5_largest_share > 0.42
  708:         ):
  709:             raise RuntimeError(
  710:                 "Visual diversity gate rejected sequence: "
  711:                 "one asset occupies "
  712:                 f"{block5_largest_share:.0%} of semantic beats; "
  713:                 "maximum is 42%."
  714:             )
  715: 
  716:         block5_visual_metrics = {
  717:             "distinct_assets": (
  718:                 block5_distinct_assets
  719:             ),
  720:             "total_beats": (
  721:                 len(block5_identities)
  722:             ),
  723:             "largest_asset_share": round(
  724:                 block5_largest_share,
  725:                 4,
  726:             ),
  727:             "max_consecutive_identical": (
  728:                 block5_max_repeat_run
  729:             ),
  730:             "quality_gate": "passed",
  731:         }
  732: 
  733:         # BLOCK5_VISUAL_QA_END
  734: 
  735:         video = await self.renderer.render(
  736:             content=generated,
  737:             scenes=storyboard,
  738:             images=render_assets,
  739:             production_package=package,
  740:             visual_beats=visual_beat_dicts,
  741:             beat_images=beat_render_assets,
  742:         )
  743: 
  744:         ####################################################
  745:         # 10. Return production evidence
  746:         ####################################################
  747:

  749:             "generated": generated,
  750:             "content_validation": validation,
  751:             "storyboard": storyboard,
  752:             "licensed_media": [

  754:                     {

--- _block5_asset_identity @ line 585 ---
  577:         # fallback image. That produced a technically valid
  578:         # 12-cut video which visually behaved like a slideshow.
  579:         #
  580:         # Rights authorization has already happened upstream.
  581:         # This gate does not authorize media and does not weaken
  582:         # any rights/evidence requirement. It only evaluates the
  583:         # final authorized beat sequence before expensive render.
  584: 
  585:         def _block5_asset_identity(asset):
  586:             if asset is None:
  587:                 return ""
  588: 
  589:             for attribute in (
  590:                 "image_path",
  591:                 "file_path",
  592:                 "local_path",
  593:                 "path",
  594:                 "source_url",
  595:                 "asset_id",
  596:             ):
  597:                 value = getattr(
  598:                     asset,
  599:                     attribute,
  600:                     None,
  601:                 )
  602: 
  603:                 if value:
  604:                     return str(value).strip().lower()
  605: 
  606:             if isinstance(asset, dict):
  607:                 for key in (
  608:                     "image_path",
  609:                     "file_path",
  610:                     "local_path",
  611:                     "path",
  612:                     "source_url",
  613:                     "asset_id",
  614:                 ):
  615:                     value = asset.get(key)
  616: 
  617:                     if value:
  618:                         return str(value).strip().lower()
  619: 
  620:             return str(asset).strip().lower()
  621: 
  622:         block5_identities = [
  623:             _block5_asset_identity(asset)
  624:             for asset in beat_render_assets
  625:         ]
  626:

  628:             not block5_identities

  276:     def _search_and_download_sync(
  277:         self,
  278:         query: str,
  279:         content_id: str,
  280:         limit: int,
  281:     ) -> list[MediaAsset]:
  282: 
  283:         search_results = self._api(
  284:             {
  285:                 "action": "query",
  286:                 "generator": "search",
  287:                 "gsrsearch": query,
  288:                 "gsrnamespace": "6",
  289:                 "gsrlimit": str(
  290:                     max(limit * 5, 10)
  291:                 ),
  292:                 "prop": "imageinfo",
  293:                 "iiprop": (
  294:                     "url|mime|extmetadata"
  295:                 ),
  296:                 "iiurlwidth": "1600",
  297:                 "format": "json",
  298:                 "formatversion": "2",
  299:             }
  300:         )
  301: 
  302:         pages = (
  303:             search_results
  304:             .get("query", {})
  305:             .get("pages", [])
  306:         )
  307: 
  308:         output_dir = (
  309:             self.base_dir
  310:             / self._safe_name(content_id)
  311:         )
  312: 
  313:         output_dir.mkdir(
  314:             parents=True,
  315:             exist_ok=True,
  316:         )
  317: 
  318:         assets: list[MediaAsset] = []
  319: 
  320:         for rank, page in enumerate(
  321:             pages,
  322:             start=1,
  323:         ):
  324: 
  325:             if len(assets) >= limit:
  326:                 break
  327: 
  328:             imageinfo = page.get(
  329:                 "imageinfo",
  330:                 [],
  331:             )
  332: 
  333:             if not imageinfo:
  334:                 continue
  335: 
  336:             info = imageinfo[0]
  337: 
  338:             mime = str(
  339:                 info.get("mime", "")
  340:             ).lower()
  341: 
  342:             if mime not in self.ALLOWED_MIME_TYPES:
  343:                 continue
  344: 
  345:             metadata = info.get(
  346:                 "extmetadata",
  347:                 {},
  348:             )
  349: 
  350:             license_name = self._meta(
  351:                 metadata,
  352:                 "LicenseShortName",
  353:             )
  354: 
  355:             usage_terms = self._meta(
  356:                 metadata,
  357:                 "UsageTerms",
  358:             )
  359: 
  360:             license_text = (
  361:                 f"{license_name} {usage_terms}"
  362:             ).lower()
  363: 
  364:             if not self._license_allowed(
  365:                 license_text
  366:             ):
  367:                 continue
  368: 
  369:             source_url = str(
  370:                 info.get(
  371:                     "descriptionurl",
  372:                     "",
  373:                 )
  374:                 or info.get(
  375:                     "url",
  376:                     "",
  377:                 )
  378:             ).strip()
  379: 
  380:             if mime.startswith("video/"):
  381:                 # Video assets must download the actual video,
  382:                 # never the image thumbnail.
  383:                 media_url = str(
  384:                     info.get(
  385:                         "url",
  386:                         "",
  387:                     )
  388:                 ).strip()
  389:             else:
  390:                 # Images use a smaller Wikimedia thumbnail
  391:                 # when available to reduce bandwidth/rate limits.
  392:                 media_url = str(
  393:                     info.get(
  394:                         "thumburl",
  395:                         "",
  396:                     )
  397:                     or info.get(
  398:                         "url",
  399:                         "",
  400:                     )
  401:                 ).strip()
  402: 
  403:             if not media_url:
  404:                 continue
  405: 
  406:             title = str(
  407:                 page.get(
  408:                     "title",
  409:                     "media",
  410:                 )
  411:             )
  412: 
  413:             relevance = self._relevance_score(
  414:                 query=query,
  415:                 title=title,
  416:                 rank=rank,
  417:             )
  418: 
  419:             if relevance < 65.0:
  420:                 continue
  421: 
  422:             suffix = self._suffix_from_url(
  423:                 media_url,
  424:                 mime,
  425:             )
  426: 
  427:             filename = (
  428:                 f"{uuid4().hex[:12]}"
  429:                 f"{suffix}"
  430:             )
  431: 
  432:             destination = (
  433:                 output_dir / filename
  434:             )
  435: 
  436:             try:
  437:                 self._download(
  438:                     media_url,
  439:                     destination,
  440:                 )
  441: 
  442:             except urllib.error.HTTPError as exc:
  443: 
  444:                 if exc.code == 429:
  445:                     # One throttled asset must not abort the
  446:                     # entire production cycle. Continue searching
  447:                     # for another explicitly licensed candidate.
  448:                     continue
  449: 
  450:                 raise
  451: 
  452:             except Exception:
  453:                 # Media-provider failure is isolated to this
  454:                 # candidate. Other search results may still work.
  455:                 continue
  456: 
  457:             creator = self._clean_html(
  458:                 self._meta(
  459:                     metadata,
  460:                     "Artist",
  461:                 )
  462:             )
  463: 
  464:             license_url = self._meta(
  465:                 metadata,
  466:                 "LicenseUrl",
  467:             )
  468: 
  469:             attribution_required = not (
  470:                 "public domain"
  471:                 in license_text
  472:                 or "cc0"
  473:                 in license_text
  474:             )
  475: 
  476:             asset_type = (
  477:                 "video"
  478:                 if mime.startswith("video/")
  479:                 else "image"
  480:             )
  481: 
  482:             assets.append(
  483:                 MediaAsset(
  484:                     asset_id=uuid4().hex,
  485:                     asset_type=asset_type,
  486:                     file_path=str(
  487:                         destination
  488:                     ),
  489:                     source_url=source_url,
  490:                     source_name=(
  491:                         "Wikimedia Commons"
  492:                     ),
  493:                     creator=creator or None,
  494:                     license_name=(
  495:                         license_name
  496:                         or usage_terms
  497:                         or None
  498:                     ),
  499:                     license_url=(
  500:                         license_url or None
  501:                     ),
  502:                     attribution_required=(
  503:                         attribution_required
  504:                     ),
  505:                     commercial_use_allowed=True,
  506:                     relevance_score=relevance,
  507:                     content_id=content_id,
  508:                 )
  509:             )
  510: 
  511:         return assets

  514:         self,
  515:         params: dict[str, str],
  516:     ) -> dict:
  517:         """
  518:         Wikimedia API request with bounded handling for
  519:         HTTP 429 and temporary transport failures.

--- WikimediaCommonsProvider._api @ line 513 ---
  505:                     commercial_use_allowed=True,
  506:                     relevance_score=relevance,

  512: 
  513:     def _api(
  514:         self,
  515:         params: dict[str, str],
  516:     ) -> dict:
  517:         """
  518:         Wikimedia API request with bounded handling for
  519:         HTTP 429 and temporary transport failures.
  520: 
  521:         Persistent provider throttling becomes an empty
  522:         provider result instead of terminating the full
  523:         video pipeline.
  524: 
  525:         Licensing, relevance and authorization logic
  526:         remain unchanged elsewhere.
  527:         """
  528: 
  529:         url = (
  530:             self.API_URL
  531:             + "?"
  532:             + urllib.parse.urlencode(
  533:                 params
  534:             )
  535:         )
  536: 
  537:         attempts = 3
  538: 
  539:         for attempt in range(
  540:             1,
  541:             attempts + 1,
  542:         ):
  543: 
  544:             request = urllib.request.Request(
  545:                 url,
  546:                 headers={
  547:                     "User-Agent": self.USER_AGENT,
  548:                 },
  549:             )
  550: 
  551:             try:
  552: 
  553:                 with urllib.request.urlopen(
  554:                     request,
  555:                     timeout=30,
  556:                 ) as response:
  557: 
  558:                     return json.loads(
  559:                         response.read().decode(
  560:                             "utf-8"
  561:                         )
  562:                     )
  563: 
  564:             except urllib.error.HTTPError as exc:
  565: 
  566:                 if exc.code != 429:
  567:                     raise
  568: 
  569:                 if attempt >= attempts:
  570:                     return {}
  571: 
  572:                 retry_after = None
  573: 
  574:                 if exc.headers is not None:
  575: 
  576:                     try:
  577:                         retry_after = (
  578:                             exc.headers.get(
  579:                                 "Retry-After"
  580:                             )
  581:                         )
  582:                     except Exception:
  583:                         retry_after = None
  584: 
  585:                 wait_seconds = None
  586: 
  587:                 if retry_after:
  588: 
  589:                     try:
  590:                         wait_seconds = float(
  591:                             str(
  592:                                 retry_after
  593:                             ).strip()
  594:                         )

  644:

  649: 
  650:         for attempt in range(
  651:             1,
  652:             attempts + 1,
  653:         ):

  656:                 url,
  657:                 headers={
  658:                     "User-Agent": self.USER_AGENT,
  659:                 },
  660:             )
  661: 
  662:             try:
  663: 
  664:                 with urllib.request.urlopen(
  665:                     request,
  666:                     timeout=60,
  667:                 ) as response:
  668: 
  669:                     destination.write_bytes(
  670:                         response.read()
  671:                     )
  672: 
  673:                 # Small courtesy delay between successful
  674:                 # Wikimedia media requests.
  675:                 time.sleep(1.25)
  676: 
  677:                 return
  678: 
  679:             except urllib.error.HTTPError as exc:

  682:                     exc.code == 429
  683:                     and attempt < attempts
  684:                 ):
  685: 
  686:                     retry_after = (
  687:                         exc.headers.get(
  688:                             "Retry-After"
  689:                         )
  690:                         if exc.headers
  691:                         else None
  692:                     )
  693: 
  694:                     try:
  695:                         delay = float(
  696:                             retry_after
  697:                         )
  698:                     except (
  699:                         TypeError,
  700:                         ValueError,
  701:                     ):

  703:                             3.0 * attempt,
  704:                             10.0,
  705:                         )

  707:                     time.sleep(
  708:                         max(delay, 2.0)
  709:                     )
  710: 
  711:                     continue
  712:

  714: 
  715:         raise RuntimeError(
  716:             "Wikimedia media download failed "

  814:     @staticmethod
  815:     def _relevance_score(
  816:         *,
  817:         query: str,
  818:         title: str,
  819:         rank: int,
  820:     ) -> float:
  821: 
  822:         stopwords = {
  823:             "the",
  824:             "a",
  825:             "an",
  826:             "and",
  827:             "or",
  828:             "of",
  829:             "to",
  830:             "in",
  831:             "on",
  832:             "for",
  833:             "how",
  834:             "why",
  835:             "what",
  836:             "is",
  837:             "are",
  838:         }
  839: 
  840:         query_words = {
  841:             word
  842:             for word in re.findall(
  843:                 r"[a-z0-9]+",
  844:                 query.lower(),
  845:             )
  846:             if (
  847:                 len(word) >= 3
  848:                 and word not in stopwords
  849:             )
  850:         }
  851: 
  852:         title_words = set(
  853:             re.findall(
  854:                 r"[a-z0-9]+",
  855:                 title.lower(),
  856:             )
  857:         )
  858: 
  859:         overlap = len(
  860:             query_words & title_words
  861:         )
  862: 
  863:         if not query_words:
  864:             semantic = 0.0
  865:         else:
  866:             semantic = (
  867:                 overlap
  868:                 / len(query_words)
  869:             )
  870: 
  871:         rank_bonus = max(
  872:             0.0,
  873:             18.0 - (rank - 1) * 1.5,
  874:         )
  875: 
  876:         return min(
  877:             100.0,
  878:             60.0
  879:             + semantic * 30.0
  880:             + rank_bonus,
  881:         )
  882:

  257:     def _scene_boundaries(
  258:         self,
  259:         source: Path,
  260:     ) -> list[float]:
  261: 
  262:         ####################################################
  263:         # FFmpeg prints scene-change timestamps through
  264:         # showinfo. We only use these as candidate boundaries.
  265:         ####################################################
  266: 
  267:         filter_value = (
  268:             "select="
  269:             f"'gt(scene,{self.SCENE_THRESHOLD})',"
  270:             "showinfo"
  271:         )
  272: 
  273:         command = [
  274:             self.ffmpeg,
  275:             "-hide_banner",
  276:             "-i",
  277:             str(source),
  278:             "-vf",
  279:             filter_value,
  280:             "-an",
  281:             "-f",
  282:             "null",
  283:             "-",
  284:         ]
  285: 
  286:         try:
  287: 
  288:             completed = subprocess.run(
  289:                 command,
  290:                 stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL,
  291:                 stderr=subprocess.PIPE,
  292:                 text=True,
  293:                 check=False,
  294:                 timeout=180,
  295:             )
  296: 
  297:         except Exception:
  298:             return []
  299: 
  300:         matches = re.findall(
  301:             r"pts_time:([0-9]+(?:\.[0-9]+)?)",
  302:             completed.stderr,
  303:         )
  304: 
  305:         values = []
  306: 
  307:         for item in matches:
  308: 
  309:             try:
  310:                 value = float(
  311:                     item
  312:                 )
  313:             except ValueError:
  314:                 continue
  315: 
  316:             if value > 0:
  317:                 values.append(
  318:                     value
  319:                 )
  320: 
  321:         return sorted(
  322:             set(values)
  323:         )
  324:

  325:     def _build_windows(
  326:         self,
  327:         *,
  328:         duration: float,
  329:         boundaries: list[float],
  330:     ) -> list[tuple[float, float]]:
  331: 
  332:         ####################################################
  333:         # Prefer real scene boundaries.
  334:         ####################################################
  335: 
  336:         points = [
  337:             0.0,
  338:             *[
  339:                 value
  340:                 for value in boundaries
  341:                 if (
  342:                     0.0 < value < duration
  343:                 )
  344:             ],
  345:             duration,
  346:         ]
  347: 
  348:         windows: list[
  349:             tuple[float, float]
  350:         ] = []
  351: 
  352:         for index in range(
  353:             len(points) - 1
  354:         ):
  355: 
  356:             start = points[index]
  357:             stop = points[index + 1]
  358: 
  359:             scene_duration = (
  360:                 stop - start
  361:             )
  362: 
  363:             if (
  364:                 scene_duration
  365:                 < self.MIN_CLIP_DURATION
  366:             ):
  367:                 continue
  368: 
  369:             current = start
  370: 
  371:             while current < stop:
  372: 
  373:                 remaining = (
  374:                     stop - current
  375:                 )
  376: 
  377:                 if (
  378:                     remaining
  379:                     < self.MIN_CLIP_DURATION
  380:                 ):
  381:                     break
  382: 
  383:                 clip_duration = min(
  384:                     self.TARGET_CLIP_DURATION,
  385:                     remaining,
  386:                     self.MAX_CLIP_DURATION,
  387:                 )
  388: 
  389:                 end = min(
  390:                     current
  391:                     + clip_duration,
  392:                     stop,
  393:                 )
  394: 
  395:                 if (
  396:                     end - current
  397:                     >= self.MIN_CLIP_DURATION
  398:                 ):
  399:                     windows.append(
  400:                         (
  401:                             current,
  402:                             end,
  403:                         )
  404:                     )
  405: 
  406:                 current = end
  407: 
  408:         ####################################################
  409:         # Some videos contain very few detectable cuts.
  410:         # Fall back to uniform windows rather than producing
  411:         # an empty index.
  412:         ####################################################
  413: 
  414:         if len(windows) < 4:
  415: 
  416:             windows = []
  417: 
  418:             current = 0.0
  419: 
  420:             while (
  421:                 current
  422:                 < duration
  423:                 and len(windows)
  424:                 < self.MAX_CLIPS_PER_ASSET
  425:             ):
  426: 
  427:                 end = min(
  428:                     current
  429:                     + self.TARGET_CLIP_DURATION,
  430:                     duration,
  431:                 )
  432: 
  433:                 if (
  434:                     end - current
  435:                     < self.MIN_CLIP_DURATION
  436:                 ):
  437:                     break
  438: 
  439:                 windows.append(
  440:                     (
  441:                         current,
  442:                         end,
  443:                     )
  444:                 )
  445: 
  446:                 current = end
  447: 
  448:         return windows
  449: 
  450:     def _extract_frame(
  451:         self,
  452:         *,
  453:         source: Path,
  454:         timestamp: float,
  455:         output: Path,
  456:     ) -> bool:

   92: 
   93:         if not beats or not clips:

  100:

  124:             embeddings,

  165:                 )
  166:

  168:                     negative_embedding
  169:                     @ image_matrix.T
  170:                 ).squeeze(0)
  171: 
  172:             final_scores = (
  173:                 positive_scores
  174:                 - (
  175:                     negative_scores
  176:                     * self.NEGATIVE_WEIGHT
  177:                 )
  178:             )
  179: 
  180:             ranked = torch.argsort(
  181:                 final_scores,
  182:                 descending=True,

   66:             )
   67: 
   68:         clips = []
   69:         opened_sources = []
   70: 
   71:         try:
   72: 
   73:             for index, match in enumerate(
   74:                 matches
   75:             ):
   76:

  105:                     ),

  107:

  112:                     start,
  113:                     end,

  137:                     clip = clip.resized(

  139:                     )
  140: 
  141:                 elif (
  142:                     profile.zoom_enabled

  145:                     clip = clip.resized(

  172:                 else "_"
  173:                 for c in title
  174:             )[:70]
  175: 
  176:             output = (
  177:                 self.output_dir
  178:                 / (
  179:                     f"{safe_name}_"
  180:                     f"{profile.format_name}_"
  181:                     "matched.mp4"
  182:                 )
  183:             )
  184: 
  185:             final.write_videofile(
  186:                 str(output),
  187:                 fps=profile.fps,
  188:                 codec="libx264",
  189:                 audio_codec="aac",
  190:                 logger=None,
  191:             )
  192: 
  193:             duration = float(
  194:                 final.duration
  195:             )
  196: 
  197:             final.close()
  198: 
  199:             return {
  200:                 "status": "success",
  201:                 "video_path": str(
  202:                     output
  203:                 ),
  204:                 "format_name": (
  205:                     profile.format_name
  206:                 ),
  207:                 "clip_count": len(
  208:                     clips
  209:                 ),
  210:                 "duration": round(
  211:                     duration,
  212:                     2,
  213:                 ),
  214:                 "resolution": (
  215:                     f"{profile.width}"
  216:                     f"x{profile.height}"
  217:                 ),
  218:                 "fps": profile.fps,
  219:             }
  220: 
  221:         finally:
  222: 
  223:             for clip in clips:
  224: 
  225:                 try:
  226:                     clip.close()
  227:                 except Exception:
  228:                     pass
  229: 
  230:             for source in opened_sources:
  231: 
  232:                 try:
  233:                     source.close()
  234:                 except Exception:
  235:                     pass

  377:                 "Storyboard/media group mismatch."

  379:         ####################################################

  391:         beat_media_evidence = []

  394:         semantic_enrichment_successes = 0
  395:         semantic_fallback_count = 0
  396: 
  397:         for beat in visual_beats:

  399:             narration_index = int(
  400:                 beat.narration_index
  401:             )
  402: 
  403:             stage_index = max(

  407:                     len(media_groups) - 1,
  408:                 ),
  409:             )
  410: 
  411:             fallback_asset = next(
  412:                 (
  413:                     asset
  414:                     for asset in media_groups[stage_index]
  415:                     if asset.asset_type == "image"

  417:                 None,
  418:             )
  419: 
  420:             if fallback_asset is None:
  421:                 raise RuntimeError(
  422:                     "Stage 2B fallback media missing for "

  430:                 beat.search_query

  432: 
  433:             if query and semantic_enrichment_attempts < self.MAX_SEMANTIC_MEDIA_ENRICHMENTS:
  434: 
  435:                 semantic_enrichment_attempts += 1
  436: 
  437:                 try:
  438: 
  439:                     found = await (
  440:                         self.media_provider
  441:                         .search_and_download(
  442:                             query=query,
  443:                             content_id=content_id,
  444:                             limit=1,
  445:                         )
  446:                     )
  447: 
  448:                     if found:
  449: 
  450:                         try:
  451: 
  452:                             authorized = (
  453:                                 self.asset_collector.authorize(
  454:                                     found,
  455:                                     content_id=content_id,
  456:                                 )
  457:                             )
  458: 
  459:                         except RuntimeError:
  460: 
  461:                             authorized = []
  462: 
  463:                         candidate = next(
  464:                             (
  465:                                 asset
  466:                                 for asset in authorized
  467:                                 if asset.asset_type == "image"
  468:                             ),
  469:                             None,
  470:                         )
  471: 
  472:                         if candidate is not None:
  473: 
  474:                             selected_asset = candidate
  475:                             selected_via = "semantic_query"
  476:                             semantic_enrichment_successes += 1
  477: 
  478:                 except Exception:
  479: 
  480:                     # Beat enrichment must never make a valid
  481:                     # production fail. The original authorized
  482:                     # scene asset remains the fallback.
  483:                     selected_asset = fallback_asset
  484:                     selected_via = "stage_fallback"
  485: 
  486:             if selected_via == "stage_fallback":
  487:                 semantic_fallback_count += 1
  488: 
  489:             beat_render_assets.append(
  490:                 selected_asset
  491:             )
  492: 
  493:             beat_media_evidence.append(
  494:                 {
  495:                     "beat_id": beat.beat_id,
  496:                     "narration_index": narration_index,
  497:                     "purpose": beat.purpose,
  498:                     "query": query,
  499:                     "selected_via": selected_via,
  500:                     "asset_id": selected_asset.asset_id,
  501:                     "source_name": selected_asset.source_name,
  502:                     "source_url": selected_asset.source_url,
  503:                     "license_name": selected_asset.license_name,
  504:                     "commercial_use_allowed": (
  505:                         selected_asset.commercial_use_allowed
  506:                     ),
  507:                 }
  508:             )
  509: 
  510:         if (
  511:             len(beat_render_assets)
  512:             != len(visual_beats)
  513:         ):
  514:             raise RuntimeError(
  515:                 "Visual beat/media timeline mismatch."
  516:             )
  517: 
  518:         ####################################################
  519:         # 7. Temporary renderer compatibility
  520:         #
  521:         # The existing renderer still accepts one image
  522:         # object per narration scene. For this integration
  523:         # test we pass the strongest authorized IMAGE from
  524:         # each group.
  525:         #
  526:         # The next renderer patch will consume the complete
  527:         # media_groups structure and produce multiple cuts.
  528:         ####################################################
  529: 
  530:         render_assets = []
  531: 
  532:         for index, group in enumerate(
  533:             media_groups,
  534:             start=1,
  535:         ):
  536: 
  537:             image_asset = next(
  538:                 (
  539:                     asset
  540:                     for asset in group
  541:                     if asset.asset_type
  542:                     == "image"
  543:                 ),
  544:                 None,
  545:             )
  546: 
  547:             if image_asset is None:
  548:                 raise RuntimeError(
  549:                     "Scene "
  550:                     f"{index} has no image-compatible "

  554:             render_assets.append(
  555:                 image_asset
  556:             )
  557: 
  558:         ####################################################
  559:         # 8. Build production package
  560:         ####################################################
  561: 
  562:         package = await self.package_builder.build(
  563:             content=generated,
  564:             scenes=storyboard,
  565:             images=render_assets,
  566:             trend=trend,
  567:         )
  568: 
  569:         ####################################################
  570:         # 9. Render
  571:         ####################################################
  572: 
  573:         # BLOCK5_VISUAL_QA_BEGIN
  574:         #
  575:         # The previous Stage2B implementation could legally
  576:         # resolve many semantic beats to the same authorized
  577:         # fallback image. That produced a technically valid
  578:         # 12-cut video which visually behaved like a slideshow.
  579:         #
  580:         # Rights authorization has already happened upstream.
  581:         # This gate does not authorize media and does not weaken
  582:         # any rights/evidence requirement. It only evaluates the
  583:         # final authorized beat sequence before expensive render.
  584: 
  585:         def _block5_asset_identity(asset):
  586:             if asset is None:
  587:                 return ""
  588: 
  589:             for attribute in (
  590:                 "image_path",
  591:                 "file_path",
  592:                 "local_path",
  593:                 "path",
  594:                 "source_url",
  595:                 "asset_id",
  596:             ):
  597:                 value = getattr(
  598:                     asset,
  599:                     attribute,
  600:                     None,
  601:                 )
  602: 
  603:                 if value:
  604:                     return str(value).strip().lower()
  605: 
  606:             if isinstance(asset, dict):
  607:                 for key in (
  608:                     "image_path",
  609:                     "file_path",
  610:                     "local_path",
  611:                     "path",
  612:                     "source_url",
  613:                     "asset_id",
  614:                 ):
  615:                     value = asset.get(key)
  616: 
  617:                     if value:
  618:                         return str(value).strip().lower()
  619: 
  620:             return str(asset).strip().lower()
  621: 
  622:         block5_identities = [
  623:             _block5_asset_identity(asset)
  624:             for asset in beat_render_assets
  625:         ]
  626: 
  627:         if (
  628:             not block5_identities
  629:             or len(block5_identities)
  630:             != len(visual_beat_dicts)
  631:         ):
  632:             raise RuntimeError(
  633:                 "Visual QA rejected semantic sequence: "
  634:                 "beat/image coverage mismatch."
  635:             )
  636: 
  637:         block5_counts = {}
  638: 
  639:         for identity in block5_identities:
  640:             if not identity:
  641:                 raise RuntimeError(
  642:                     "Visual QA rejected semantic sequence: "
  643:                     "unidentifiable authorized asset."
  644:                 )
  645: 
  646:             block5_counts[identity] = (
  647:                 block5_counts.get(identity, 0)
  648:                 + 1
  649:             )
  650: 
  651:         block5_distinct_assets = len(
  652:             block5_counts
  653:         )
  654: 
  655:         block5_largest_share = (
  656:             max(block5_counts.values())
  657:             / len(block5_identities)
  658:         )
  659: 
  660:         block5_max_repeat_run = 1
  661:         block5_current_repeat_run = 1
  662: 
  663:         for block5_index in range(
  664:             1,
  665:             len(block5_identities),
  666:         ):
  667:             if (
  668:                 block5_identities[block5_index]
  669:                 == block5_identities[
  670:                     block5_index - 1
  671:                 ]
  672:             ):
  673:                 block5_current_repeat_run += 1
  674:                 block5_max_repeat_run = max(
  675:                     block5_max_repeat_run,
  676:                     block5_current_repeat_run,
  677:                 )
  678:             else:
  679:                 block5_current_repeat_run = 1
  680: 
  681:         block5_minimum_distinct = min(
  682:             4,
  683:             len(block5_identities),
  684:         )
  685: 
  686:         if (
  687:             block5_distinct_assets
  688:             < block5_minimum_distinct
  689:         ):
  690:             raise RuntimeError(
  691:                 "Visual diversity gate rejected sequence: "
  692:                 f"{block5_distinct_assets} distinct assets "
  693:                 f"for {len(block5_identities)} beats; "
  694:                 f"minimum is {block5_minimum_distinct}."
  695:             )
  696: 
  697:         if block5_max_repeat_run > 2:
  698:             raise RuntimeError(
  699:                 "Visual diversity gate rejected sequence: "
  700:                 f"same asset appears for "
  701:                 f"{block5_max_repeat_run} consecutive beats; "
  702:                 "maximum is 2."
  703:             )
  704: 
  705:         if (
  706:             len(block5_identities) >= 8
  707:             and block5_largest_share > 0.42
  708:         ):
  709:             raise RuntimeError(
  710:                 "Visual diversity gate rejected sequence: "
  711:                 "one asset occupies "
  712:                 f"{block5_largest_share:.0%} of semantic beats; "
  713:                 "maximum is 42%."
  714:             )
  715: 
  716:         block5_visual_metrics = {
  717:             "distinct_assets": (
  718:                 block5_distinct_assets
  719:             ),
  720:             "total_beats": (
  721:                 len(block5_identities)
  722:             ),
  723:             "largest_asset_share": round(
  724:                 block5_largest_share,
  725:                 4,
  726:             ),
  727:             "max_consecutive_identical": (
  728:                 block5_max_repeat_run
  729:             ),
  730:             "quality_gate": "passed",
  731:         }
  732: 
  733:         # BLOCK5_VISUAL_QA_END
  734: 
  735:         video = await self.renderer.render(
  736:             content=generated,
  737:             scenes=storyboard,
  738:             images=render_assets,
  739:             production_package=package,
  740:             visual_beats=visual_beat_dicts,
  741:             beat_images=beat_render_assets,

  204:                         content

  207:                         content

  211:             # for the first Rich V1 integration test.

  213:                 break

  216:             pack.set_summary(

  218:                     all_content

  247:     def _research_queries(
  248:         self,
  249:         topic: str,
  250:     ) -> list[str]:
  251:         clean = " ".join(
  252:             str(topic).split()
  253:         ).strip()
  254: 
  255:         if not clean:
  256:             return []
  257: 
  258:         lower = clean.lower()
  259: 
  260:         exact_queries: list[str] = [clean]
  261:         concept_queries: list[str] = []
  262:         alias_queries: list[str] = []
  263: 
  264:         try:
  265:             concept_queries = list(
  266:                 self._expand_concept_queries(clean)
  267:                 or []
  268:             )
  269:         except Exception:
  270:             concept_queries = []
  271: 
  272:         for trigger, aliases in self.RESEARCH_ALIASES.items():
  273:             if trigger in lower:
  274:                 alias_queries.extend(
  275:                     str(alias).strip()
  276:                     for alias in aliases
  277:                     if str(alias).strip()
  278:                 )
  279: 
  280:         # The exact premise must remain the highest-priority
  281:         # research request. Previously aliases could replace it
  282:         # completely, which encouraged broad background evidence.
  283:         ordered = (
  284:             exact_queries
  285:             + concept_queries
  286:             + alias_queries
  287:         )
  288: 
  289:         generic_terms = {
  290:             "film",
  291:             "filmmaking",
  292:             "cinema",
  293:             "technology",
  294:             "science",
  295:             "engineering",
  296:             "business",
  297:             "history",
  298:             "movie",
  299:         }
  300: 
  301:         topic_tokens = {
  302:             token.strip(
  303:                 ".,:;!?()[]{}\"'"
  304:             ).lower()
  305:             for token in clean.split()
  306:             if len(
  307:                 token.strip(
  308:                     ".,:;!?()[]{}\"'"
  309:                 )
  310:             ) >= 4
  311:         }
  312: 
  313:         def query_quality(query: str) -> tuple:
  314:             normalized = " ".join(
  315:                 str(query).split()
  316:             ).strip()
  317: 
  318:             tokens = {
  319:                 token.strip(
  320:                     ".,:;!?()[]{}\"'"
  321:                 ).lower()
  322:                 for token in normalized.split()
  323:                 if len(
  324:                     token.strip(
  325:                         ".,:;!?()[]{}\"'"
  326:                     )
  327:                 ) >= 4
  328:             }
  329: 
  330:             overlap = len(
  331:                 tokens & topic_tokens
  332:             )
  333: 
  334:             exact = int(
  335:                 normalized.lower()
  336:                 == clean.lower()
  337:             )
  338: 
  339:             generic_only = int(
  340:                 bool(tokens)
  341:                 and tokens.issubset(generic_terms)
  342:             )
  343: 
  344:             return (
  345:                 exact,
  346:                 overlap,
  347:                 -generic_only,
  348:                 min(len(tokens), 12),
  349:             )
  350: 
  351:         seen: set[str] = set()
  352:         unique: list[str] = []
  353: 
  354:         for query in ordered:
  355:             normalized = " ".join(
  356:                 str(query).split()
  357:             ).strip()
  358: 
  359:             if not normalized:
  360:                 continue
  361: 
  362:             key = normalized.casefold()
  363: 
  364:             if key in seen:
  365:                 continue
  366: 
  367:             seen.add(key)
  368:             unique.append(normalized)
  369: 
  370:         if not unique:
  371:             return [clean]
  372: 
  373:         exact = unique[0]
  374: 
  375:         remainder = sorted(
  376:             unique[1:],
  377:             key=query_quality,
  378:             reverse=True,
  379:         )
  380: 
  381:         # Keep research bounded. Exact premise + up to four
  382:         # supporting concept/alias queries.
  383:         return [exact] + remainder[:4]

   15:     This is separate from the trend RSS provider because its job is
   16:     to gather research material rather than rank trends.
   17:     """
   18: 
   19:     @property
   20:     def name(self) -> str:
   21:         return "Google News Research"
   22: 
   23:     def research(
   24:         self,
   25:         topic: str,
   26:     ) -> list[dict]:
   27:         if not topic or not topic.strip():
   28:             return []
   29: 
   30:         url = (
   31:             "https://news.google.com/rss/search"
   32:             f"?q={self._encode_query(topic)}"
   33:             "&hl=en-IN"
   34:             "&gl=IN"
   35:             "&ceid=IN:en"
   36:         )
   37: 
   38:         request = Request(
   39:             url,
   40:             headers={
   41:                 "User-Agent": (
   42:                     "Mozilla/5.0 "
   43:                     "(Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64) "
   44:                     "AppleWebKit/537.36 "
   45:                     "(KHTML, like Gecko) "
   46:                     "Chrome/131.0 Safari/537.36"
   47:                 )
   48:             },
   49:         )
   50: 
   51:         try:
   52:             with urlopen(
   53:                 request,
   54:                 timeout=15,
   55:             ) as response:
   56:                 xml_data = response.read()
   57: 
   58:             root = ElementTree.fromstring(
   59:                 xml_data
   60:             )
   61: 
   62:         except Exception as exc:
   63:             print(
   64:                 f"[NewsProvider] Failed to research "
   65:                 f"'{topic}': {exc}"
   66:             )
   67:             return []
   68: 
   69:         results = []
   70: 
   71:         for item in root.findall(".//item"):
   72:             title = self._text(
   73:                 item.find("title")
   74:             )
   75:             link = self._text(
   76:                 item.find("link")
   77:             )
   78:             description = self._clean_text(
   79:                 self._text(
   80:                     item.find("description")
   81:                 )
   82:             )
   83:             published = self._text(
   84:                 item.find("pubDate")
   85:             )
   86: 
   87:             if not title:
   88:                 continue
   89: 
   90:             publisher = self._extract_publisher(
   91:                 title
   92:             )
   93: 
   94:             headline = self._extract_headline(
   95:                 title
   96:             )
   97: 
   98:             content_parts = []
   99: 
  100:             if description:
  101:                 content_parts.append(description)
  102: 
  103:             if published:
  104:                 content_parts.append(
  105:                     f"Published: {published}"
  106:                 )
  107: 
  108:             results.append(
  109:                 {
  110:                     "title": headline,
  111:                     "content": "\n".join(
  112:                         content_parts
  113:                     ),
  114:                     "source": "Google News",
  115:                     "publisher": publisher,
  116:                     "url": link,
  117:                     "published": published,
  118:                     "confidence": 0.80,
  119:                 }
  120:             )
  121: 
  122:             if len(results) >= 5:
  123:                 break
  124: 
  125:         print(
  126:             f"[NewsProvider] Retrieved "
  127:             f"{len(results)} research results "
  128:             f"for '{topic}'"
  129:         )
  130: 
  131:         return results

    5: from backend.services.research.confidence import ResearchConfidenceScorer
    6: from backend.services.research.knowledge_pack import KnowledgePack
    7: from backend.services.research.providers.news import NewsProvider
    8: from backend.services.research.providers.registry import ResearchRegistry
    9: from backend.services.research.providers.wikipedia import WikipediaProvider
   10: from backend.services.research.research_memory import ResearchMemory
   11: 
   12: 
   13: def sanitize_topic(raw_title: str) -> str:
   14:     """
   15:     Extract the core topic from noisy titles for better research quality.
   16:     """
   17: 
   18:     if not raw_title:
   19:         return ""
   20: 
   21:     segments = re.split(r"[|\-–—]", raw_title)
   22:     clean = segments[0] if segments else raw_title
   23: 
   24:     noise_patterns = [
   25:         r"\b(official|teaser|trailer|hd|video|full movie|breaking news|live)\b",
   26:         r"\b(ndtv|live law|the hindu|times of india|hindustan times)\b",
   27:         r"\b(\d{1,2}(st|nd|rd|th)?\s+(jan|feb|mar|apr|may|jun|jul|aug|sep|sept|oct|nov|dec))\b",
   28:         r"\b202\d\b",
   29:     ]
   30: 
   31:     for pattern in noise_patterns:
   32:         clean = re.sub(
   33:             pattern,
   34:             "",
   35:             clean,
   36:             flags=re.IGNORECASE,
   37:         )
   38: 
   39:     clean = re.sub(r"\s+", " ", clean).strip()
   40: 
   41:     return clean if len(clean) >= 3 else raw_title.strip()
   42:

  124:         )
  125:

  127: 
  128:         # Save for future requests
  129:         self.memory.save(
  130:             clean_query,
  131:             pack,
  132:         )
  133:

  164: 
  165:     @staticmethod
  166:     def _calculate_research_score(pack: KnowledgePack) -> float:
  167:         """
  168:         Simple research quality score.
  169: 
  170:         This will later evolve into Jarvis'
  171:         research confidence system.
  172:         """
  173: 
  174:         score = 0.0
  175: 
  176:         score += min(len(pack.sources) * 2.0, 20.0)
  177: 
  178:         score += min(len(pack.facts), 10)
  179: 
  180:         if pack.summary:
  181:             score += 10
  182: 
  183:         return round(score, 2)

  178:                     retry_data

  184:                     "length recovery.",
  185:                     retry_words,

  205:                         self._recover_generation_length(
  206:                             data=retry_data,

  234:                 topic=topic,
  235:                 research=research,

  237: 
  238:         ####################################################
  239:         # Normalize

  243:             data.get(
  244:                 "title",
  245:                 topic,
  246:             )

  250:             str(tag).strip()

  253:                 []

  256:         ]
  257:

  259:             str(line).strip()

  261:                 "script_lines",
  262:                 []
  263:             )
  264:             if str(line).strip()
  265:         ]
  266: 
  267:         if len(lines) != 4:
  268:             raise RuntimeError(
  269:                 "Generated content normalization produced "
  270:                 f"{len(lines)} script lines; expected exactly 4."
  271:             )
  272: 
  273:         lines = lines[:4]
  274: 
  275:         if format_name == "famous_movie_commentary":
  276: 
  277:             evidence_ids = data.get(
  278:                 "evidence_ids",
  279:                 [],
  280:             )
  281: 
  282:             evidence_validation = (
  283:                 self.claim_evidence_validator.validate(
  284:                     script_lines=lines,
  285:                     evidence_ids=evidence_ids,
  286:                     research=research,
  287:                 )
  288:             )
  289: 
  290:             if not evidence_validation.valid:
  291:                 raise RuntimeError(
  292:                     "Movie script failed claim-evidence validation: "
  293:                     f"{evidence_validation.issues}"
  294:                 )
  295: 
  296:         if format_name == "famous_movie_commentary":
  297: 
  298:             semantic_validation = (
  299:                 await self.semantic_claim_evidence_validator.validate(
  300:                     script_lines=lines,
  301:                     evidence_ids=data.get(
  302:                         "evidence_ids",
  303:                         [],
  304:                     ),
  305:                     research=research,
  306:                 )
  307:             )
  308: 
  309:             if not semantic_validation.valid:
  310: 
  311:                 repaired_lines = (
  312:                     await self._repair_movie_semantic_failures(
  313:                         script_lines=lines,
  314:                         evidence_ids=data.get(
  315:                             "evidence_ids",
  316:                             [],
  317:                         ),
  318:                         research=research,
  319:                         semantic_validation=semantic_validation,
  320:                         topic=topic,
  321:                         movie_title=movie_title,
  322:                     )
  323:                 )

  326:                     raise RuntimeError(
  327:                         "Movie script failed semantic evidence "
  328:                         "validation and targeted repair failed: "
  329:                         f"{semantic_validation.to_dict()}"

  340:                 )
  341: 
  342: 
  343:         ####################################################
  344:         # Final fail-closed script-length guarantee
  345:         ####################################################
  346: 
  347:         final_word_count = sum(
  348:             len(line.split())
  349:             for line in lines
  350:         )
  351: 
  352:         if not (
  353:             self.MIN_WORDS
  354:             <= final_word_count
  355:             <= self.MAX_WORDS
  356:         ):
  357:             raise RuntimeError(
  358:                 "Final generated script failed length "
  359:                 "validation after all generation and "
  360:                 "repair stages: "
  361:                 f"{final_word_count} words "
  362:                 f"(required {self.MIN_WORDS}-"
  363:                 f"{self.MAX_WORDS})."
  364:             )
  365: 
  366:         return GeneratedContent(

  368:             hashtags=hashtags,
  369:             script_lines=lines,
  370:             metadata={

  373:                 "premise_audit": self.evaluate_premise_strength(topic, reference_format),
  374:                 "content_format": format_name,
  375:                 "movie_title": movie_title,
  376:                 "evidence_ids": (
  377:                     data.get("evidence_ids", [])
  378:                     if format_name == "famous_movie_commentary"

  380:                 ),
  381:                 "word_count": sum(
  382:                     len(line.split())
  383:                     for line in lines
