  388:     ########################################################
  389:     # Content repair
  390:     ########################################################
  391: 
  392:     async def _recover_movie_generation_length(
  393:         self,
  394:         *,
  395:         data: dict,
  396:         topic: str,
  397:         movie_title: str,
  398:         research: str,
  399:     ) -> dict | None:
  400:         """
  401:         Recover short grounded movie-facts scripts.
  402: 
  403:         The research string may contain grounded facts without
  404:         explicit evidence labels. Recovery therefore creates a
  405:         deterministic local evidence contract from those facts.
  406: 
  407:         Validation remains fail-closed.
  408:         """
  409: 
  410:         if not self._is_structurally_valid_content(
  411:             data
  412:         ):
  413:             return None
  414: 
  415:         current_words = self._word_count(
  416:             data
  417:         )
  418: 
  419:         if (
  420:             self.MIN_WORDS
  421:             <= current_words
  422:             <= self.MAX_WORDS
  423:         ):
  424:             return data
  425: 
  426:         if current_words > self.MAX_WORDS:
  427:             return None
  428: 
  429:         # -------------------------------------------------
  430:         # Build deterministic evidence units.
  431:         #
  432:         # Existing labelled evidence is preserved when
  433:         # available. Otherwise grounded research sentences
  434:         # become E1, E2, ... locally for this recovery.
  435:         # -------------------------------------------------
  436: 
  437:         labelled_matches = re.findall(
  438:             r"\[(E\d+)\]\s*([^\n]+)",
  439:             research,
  440:             flags=re.IGNORECASE,
  441:         )
  442: 
  443:         evidence_map = {}
  444: 
  445:         for evidence_id, text in labelled_matches:
  446: 
  447:             normalized = (
  448:                 str(evidence_id)
  449:                 .strip()
  450:                 .upper()
  451:             )
  452: 
  453:             cleaned = " ".join(
  454:                 str(text).split()
  455:             ).strip()
  456: 
  457:             if normalized and cleaned:
  458:                 evidence_map[
  459:                     normalized
  460:                 ] = cleaned
  461: 
  462:         if not evidence_map:
  463: 
  464:             # Research supplied by EvergreenResearch currently
  465:             # arrives as grounded prose rather than E-labelled
  466:             # evidence. Split it conservatively into factual
  467:             # sentence-sized evidence units.
  468: 
  469:             raw_sentences = re.split(
  470:                 r"(?<=[.!?])\s+",
  471:                 str(research),
  472:             )
  473: 
  474:             evidence_units = []
  475: 
  476:             banned_fragments = (
  477:                 '"topic"',
  478:                 '"summary"',
  479:                 '"facts"',
  480:                 '"keywords"',
  481:                 '"sources"',
  482:                 '"score"',
  483:                 '"category"',
  484:                 '"url"',
  485:                 '"confidence"',
  486:             )
  487: 
  488:             for sentence in raw_sentences:
  489: 
  490:                 cleaned = " ".join(
  491:                     sentence.split()
  492:                 ).strip()
  493: 
  494:                 if not cleaned:
  495:                     continue
  496: 
  497:                 lower = cleaned.lower()
  498: 
  499:                 if any(
  500:                     fragment in lower
  501:                     for fragment in banned_fragments
  502:                 ):
  503:                     # JSON/key scaffolding is not evidence.
  504:                     # Content following a key can still appear
  505:                     # in later sentence units.
  506:                     continue
  507: 
  508:                 words = cleaned.split()
  509: 
  510:                 if len(words) < 5:
  511:                     continue
  512: 
  513:                 if cleaned in evidence_units:
  514:                     continue
  515: 
  516:                 evidence_units.append(
  517:                     cleaned
  518:                 )
  519: 
  520:                 if len(evidence_units) >= 12:
  521:                     break
  522: 
  523:             # If the serialized research format caused the
  524:             # conservative pass to reject everything, perform
  525:             # one bounded textual extraction rather than
  526:             # inventing evidence.
  527:             if not evidence_units:
  528: 
  529:                 candidate_text = re.sub(
  530:                     r'https?://\S+',
  531:                     ' ',
  532:                     str(research),
  533:                 )
  534: 
  535:                 candidate_text = re.sub(
  536:                     r'["{}\[\],:]',
  537:                     ' ',
  538:                     candidate_text,
  539:                 )
  540: 
  541:                 candidate_text = " ".join(
  542:                     candidate_text.split()
  543:                 )
  544: 
  545:                 raw_sentences = re.split(
  546:                     r"(?<=[.!?])\s+",
  547:                     candidate_text,
  548:                 )
  549: 
  550:                 for sentence in raw_sentences:
  551: 
  552:                     cleaned = " ".join(
  553:                         sentence.split()
  554:                     ).strip()
  555: 
  556:                     if len(
  557:                         cleaned.split()
  558:                     ) < 5:
  559:                         continue
  560: 
  561:                     if cleaned in evidence_units:
  562:                         continue
  563: 
  564:                     evidence_units.append(
  565:                         cleaned
  566:                     )
  567: 
  568:                     if len(evidence_units) >= 12:
  569:                         break
  570: 
  571:             for index, text in enumerate(
  572:                 evidence_units,
  573:                 start=1,
  574:             ):
  575:                 evidence_map[
  576:                     f"E{index}"
  577:                 ] = text
  578: 
  579:         if not evidence_map:
  580:             logger.warning(
  581:                 "Movie length recovery blocked: "
  582:                 "no grounded evidence units could be derived."
  583:             )
  584:             return None
  585: 
  586:         allowed_evidence_ids = list(
  587:             evidence_map.keys()
  588:         )
  589: 
  590:         allowed_evidence_set = set(
  591:             allowed_evidence_ids
  592:         )
  593: 
  594:         allowed_evidence_text = ", ".join(
  595:             allowed_evidence_ids
  596:         )
  597: 
  598:         evidence_packet = "\n".join(
  599:             f"[{evidence_id}] {text}"
  600:             for evidence_id, text
  601:             in evidence_map.items()
  602:         )
  603: 
  604:         current_candidate = data
  605: 
  606:         for attempt in range(1, 4):
  607: 
  608:             candidate_words = self._word_count(
  609:                 current_candidate
  610:             )
  611: 
  612:             current_json = json.dumps(
  613:                 current_candidate,
  614:                 indent=2,
  615:             )
  616: 
  617:             missing_words = max(
  618:                 0,
  619:                 85 - candidate_words,
  620:             )
  621: 
  622:             if attempt == 1:
  623:                 correction = f"""
  624: The current narration has {candidate_words} words.
  625: 
  626: Rewrite it to 85-100 narration words total.
  627: 
  628: Approximately {missing_words} additional useful words
  629: are needed if the script is short.
  630: 
  631: Add only explanation directly supported by the
  632: evidence packet below.
  633: """
  634:             else:
  635:                 correction = f"""
  636: THIS IS RECOVERY ATTEMPT {attempt} OF 3.
  637: 
  638: The previous candidate failed validation.
  639: 
  640: It contains {candidate_words} narration words.
  641: 
  642: Return 85-100 narration words.
  643: Use exactly four narration lines.
  644: Aim for 21-25 words per line.
  645: 
  646: Count the words before responding.
  647: Do not add filler.
  648: Do not invent facts.
  649: Use only the allowed evidence IDs.
  650: """
  651: 
  652:             messages = [
  653:                 {
  654:                     "role": "system",
  655:                     "content": f"""
  656: You are repairing a grounded movie-facts
  657: YouTube Short.
  658: 
  659: Return ONLY one JSON object.
  660: 
  661: Required keys:
  662: "title"
  663: "hashtags"
  664: "script_lines"
  665: "evidence_ids"
  666: 
  667: MANDATORY:
  668: 
  669: - exactly four script_lines
  670: - exactly four evidence_ids lists
  671: - 85-100 narration words total
  672: - no narration line over 29 words
  673: - natural spoken vertical-video narration
  674: - line 1 creates the information gap
  675: - line 2 gives only necessary context
  676: - line 3 gives the strongest supported mechanism,
  677:   example, action, contrast, number, or consequence
  678: - line 4 resolves the opening gap
  679: - every line adds new information
  680: - no generic CTA
  681: - no filler
  682: - no invented facts
  683: - evidence labels never appear inside narration
  684: 
  685: The ONLY legal evidence IDs are:
  686: 
  687: {allowed_evidence_text}
  688: 
  689: Every narration line must cite at least one legal
  690: evidence ID.
  691: 
  692: Never create another ID.
  693: 
  694: {correction}
  695: """,
  696:                 },
  697:                 {
  698:                     "role": "user",
  699:                     "content": f"""
  700: MOVIE:
  701: {movie_title}
  702: 
  703: TOPIC:
  704: {topic}
  705: 
  706: CURRENT SCRIPT:
  707: {current_json}
  708: 
  709: GROUNDED EVIDENCE PACKET:
  710: {evidence_packet}
  711: 
  712: Produce the corrected script.
  713: 
  714: Return JSON only.
  715: """,
  716:                 },
  717:             ]
  718: 
  719:             raw = await self.llm.chat(
  720:                 messages,
  721:                 json_mode=True,
  722:             )
  723: 
  724:             recovered = self._extract_json(
  725:                 raw
  726:             )
  727: 
  728:             if not isinstance(
  729:                 recovered,
  730:                 dict,
  731:             ):
  732:                 logger.warning(
  733:                     "Movie recovery attempt %s "
  734:                     "returned invalid JSON.",
  735:                     attempt,
  736:                 )
  737:                 continue
  738: 
  739:             if not self._is_structurally_valid_content(
  740:                 recovered
  741:             ):
  742:                 logger.warning(
  743:                     "Movie recovery attempt %s "
  744:                     "failed structural validation.",
  745:                     attempt,
  746:                 )
  747: 
  748:                 current_candidate = recovered
  749:                 continue
  750: 
  751:             recovered_words = self._word_count(
  752:                 recovered
  753:             )
  754: 
  755:             if not (
  756:                 self.MIN_WORDS
  757:                 <= recovered_words
  758:                 <= self.MAX_WORDS
  759:             ):
  760:                 logger.warning(
  761:                     "Movie recovery attempt %s "
  762:                     "failed length validation: %s words.",
  763:                     attempt,
  764:                     recovered_words,
  765:                 )
  766: 
  767:                 current_candidate = recovered
  768:                 continue
  769: 
  770:             recovered_lines = [
  771:                 str(line).strip()
  772:                 for line in recovered.get(
  773:                     "script_lines",
  774:                     []
  775:                 )
  776:             ]
  777: 
  778:             if len(recovered_lines) != 4:
  779:                 logger.warning(
  780:                     "Movie recovery attempt %s "
  781:                     "did not return four lines.",
  782:                     attempt,
  783:                 )
  784: 
  785:                 current_candidate = recovered
  786:                 continue
  787: 
  788:             if any(
  789:                 len(line.split()) > 29
  790:                 for line in recovered_lines
  791:             ):
  792:                 logger.warning(
  793:                     "Movie recovery attempt %s "
  794:                     "contains a line over 29 words.",
  795:                     attempt,
  796:                 )
  797:

  956:     async def _repair_movie_semantic_failures(

  966:         """

  970:         Every round is revalidated lexically and semantically.

  974:

 1007:             if not failed_lines:
 1008:                 return working_lines

 1023:                     line_number < 1

 1031:                         line_number - 1
 1032:                     ]

 1070:

 1072:   "repairs": [
 1073:     {
 1074:       "line": 3,

 1085: 4. Do not replace one unsupported claim with another unsupported claim.
 1086: 5. Prefer wording that closely follows the evidence.
 1087: 6. Do not strengthen the evidence.
 1088: 7. Do not simplify attribution incorrectly.
 1089: 8. If multiple studios contributed, do not attribute all work to one studio.

 1161:                     ValueError,

 1260:     def _recover_generation_length(
 1261:         self,
 1262:         data: dict,
 1263:         research: str,
 1264:     ) -> dict | None:
 1265:         """
 1266:         Expand a structurally valid but short script using only
 1267:         complete sentences already present in supplied research.
 1268:         """
 1269: 
 1270:         if not self._is_structurally_valid_content(data):
 1271:             return None
 1272: 
 1273:         current_words = self._word_count(data)
 1274: 
 1275:         if self.MIN_WORDS <= current_words <= self.MAX_WORDS:
 1276:             return data
 1277: 
 1278:         if current_words > self.MAX_WORDS:
 1279:             return None
 1280: 
 1281:         research_text = " ".join(
 1282:             str(research).split()
 1283:         ).strip()
 1284: 
 1285:         if not research_text:
 1286:             return None
 1287: 
 1288:         sentences = [
 1289:             sentence.strip()
 1290:             for sentence in re.split(
 1291:                 r"(?<=[.!?])\s+",
 1292:                 research_text,
 1293:             )
 1294:             if sentence.strip()
 1295:         ]
 1296: 
 1297:         lines = [
 1298:             str(line).strip()
 1299:             for line in data.get(
 1300:                 "script_lines",
 1301:                 []
 1302:             )
 1303:         ]
 1304: 
 1305:         if len(lines) != 4:
 1306:             return None
 1307: 
 1308:         existing_text = " ".join(lines).lower()
 1309: 
 1310:         safe_sentences = []
 1311: 
 1312:         for sentence in sentences:
 1313: 
 1314:             if len(sentence.split()) < 5:
 1315:                 continue
 1316: 
 1317:             if sentence.lower() in existing_text:
 1318:                 continue
 1319: 
 1320:             safe_sentences.append(sentence)
 1321: 
 1322:         candidate_lines = list(lines)
 1323:         total_words = current_words
 1324:         line_index = 1
 1325: 
 1326:         for sentence in safe_sentences:
 1327: 
 1328:             sentence_words = len(
 1329:                 sentence.split()
 1330:             )
 1331: 
 1332:             if (
 1333:                 total_words + sentence_words
 1334:                 > self.MAX_WORDS
 1335:             ):
 1336:                 continue
 1337: 
 1338:             base = candidate_lines[
 1339:                 line_index
 1340:             ].rstrip()
 1341: 
 1342:             if (
 1343:                 base
 1344:                 and base[-1] not in ".!?"
 1345:             ):
 1346:                 base += "."
 1347: 
 1348:             candidate_lines[
 1349:                 line_index
 1350:             ] = (
 1351:                 base
 1352:                 + " "
 1353:                 + sentence
 1354:             ).strip()
 1355: 
 1356:             total_words += sentence_words
 1357: 
 1358:             line_index += 1
 1359: 
 1360:             if line_index > 3:
 1361:                 line_index = 1
 1362: 
 1363:             if total_words >= self.MIN_WORDS:
 1364:                 break
 1365: 
 1366:         candidate = {
 1367:             **data,
 1368:             "script_lines": candidate_lines,
 1369:         }
 1370: 
 1371:         if not self._is_valid_content(
 1372:             candidate
 1373:         ):
 1374:             return None
 1375: 
 1376:         return candidate

 1644:     def _recover_repair_length(
 1645:         self,
 1646:         *,
 1647:         data: dict,
 1648:         research: str,
 1649:         banned_terms: list[str],
 1650:     ) -> dict | None:
 1651:         """
 1652:         Deterministically recover an undersized repair
 1653:         using only grounded research.
 1654: 
 1655:         Fail closed if structure, length, line limits,
 1656:         or banned-term validation cannot be satisfied.
 1657:         """
 1658: 
 1659:         if not self._is_structurally_valid_content(data):
 1660:             return None
 1661: 
 1662:         current_words = self._word_count(data)
 1663: 
 1664:         remaining_terms = self._remaining_flagged_terms(
 1665:             data=data,
 1666:             banned_terms=banned_terms,
 1667:         )
 1668: 
 1669:         if (
 1670:             self.MIN_WORDS
 1671:             <= current_words
 1672:             <= self.MAX_WORDS
 1673:             and not remaining_terms
 1674:         ):
 1675:             return data
 1676: 
 1677:         if current_words > self.MAX_WORDS:
 1678:             return None
 1679: 
 1680:         candidate = dict(data)
 1681: 
 1682:         candidate["script_lines"] = [
 1683:             str(line).strip()
 1684:             for line in data.get(
 1685:                 "script_lines",
 1686:                 [],
 1687:             )
 1688:         ]
 1689: 
 1690:         if len(candidate["script_lines"]) != 4:
 1691:             return None
 1692: 
 1693:         import re
 1694: 
 1695:         raw_sentences = re.split(
 1696:             r"(?<=[.!?])\s+",
 1697:             str(research or "").strip(),
 1698:         )
 1699: 
 1700:         additions = []
 1701: 
 1702:         existing_text = " ".join(
 1703:             candidate["script_lines"]
 1704:         ).lower()
 1705: 
 1706:         for raw_sentence in raw_sentences:
 1707: 
 1708:             sentence = " ".join(
 1709:                 str(raw_sentence).split()
 1710:             ).strip()
 1711: 
 1712:             words = sentence.split()
 1713: 
 1714:             if len(words) < 5:
 1715:                 continue
 1716: 
 1717:             if len(words) > 18:
 1718:                 continue
 1719: 
 1720:             lowered = sentence.lower()
 1721: 
 1722:             if lowered in existing_text:
 1723:                 continue
 1724: 
 1725:             flagged = False
 1726: 
 1727:             for term in banned_terms:
 1728: 
 1729:                 clean_term = str(term).strip().lower()
 1730: 
 1731:                 if clean_term and clean_term in lowered:
 1732:                     flagged = True
 1733:                     break
 1734: 
 1735:             if flagged:
 1736:                 continue
 1737: 
 1738:             additions.append(sentence)
 1739: 
 1740:         target_lines = [1, 2, 3]
 1741: 
 1742:         for addition in additions:
 1743: 
 1744:             if self._word_count(candidate) >= 85:
 1745:                 break
 1746: 
 1747:             placed = False
 1748: 
 1749:             for line_index in target_lines:
 1750: 
 1751:                 current_line = candidate[
 1752:                     "script_lines"
 1753:                 ][line_index]
 1754: 
 1755:                 combined = (
 1756:                     current_line.rstrip(" .")
 1757:                     + ". "
 1758:                     + addition
 1759:                 ).strip()
 1760: 
 1761:                 if len(combined.split()) > 29:
 1762:                     continue
 1763: 
 1764:                 trial = dict(candidate)
 1765: 
 1766:                 trial["script_lines"] = list(
 1767:                     candidate["script_lines"]
 1768:                 )
 1769: 
 1770:                 trial["script_lines"][
 1771:                     line_index
 1772:                 ] = combined
 1773: 
 1774:                 trial_words = self._word_count(
 1775:                     trial
 1776:                 )
 1777: 
 1778:                 if trial_words > self.MAX_WORDS:
 1779:                     continue
 1780: 
 1781:                 candidate = trial
 1782:                 placed = True
 1783:                 break
 1784: 
 1785:             if not placed:
 1786:                 continue
 1787: 
 1788:         if not self._is_valid_content(candidate):
 1789:             return None
 1790: 
 1791:         final_words = self._word_count(candidate)
 1792: 
 1793:         if not (
 1794:             self.MIN_WORDS
 1795:             <= final_words
 1796:             <= self.MAX_WORDS
 1797:         ):
 1798:             return None
 1799: 
 1800:         if any(
 1801:             len(str(line).split()) > 29
 1802:             for line in candidate["script_lines"]
 1803:         ):
 1804:             return None
 1805: 
 1806:         if self._remaining_flagged_terms(
 1807:             data=candidate,
 1808:             banned_terms=banned_terms,
 1809:         ):
 1810:             return None
 1811: 
 1812:         return candidate

   78:         Preserve source aspect ratio while filling the

  113:             .with_duration(max(0.05, float(duration)))

  231: 
  232:                 block5_renderer_distinct = len(
  233:                     block5_path_counts
  234:                 )
  235: 
  236:                 block5_renderer_run = 1
  237:                 block5_renderer_max_run = 1
  238: 
  239:                 for block5_index in range(
  240:                     1,
  241:                     len(block5_paths),
  242:                 ):
  243:                     if (
  244:                         block5_paths[block5_index]
  245:                         == block5_paths[
  246:                             block5_index - 1
  247:                         ]
  248:                     ):
  249:                         block5_renderer_run += 1
  250:                         block5_renderer_max_run = max(
  251:                             block5_renderer_max_run,
  252:                             block5_renderer_run,
  253:                         )
  254:                     else:
  255:                         block5_renderer_run = 1
  256: 
  257:                 block5_renderer_share = (
  258:                     max(
  259:                         block5_path_counts.values()
  260:                     )
  261:                     / len(block5_paths)
  262:                 )
  263: 
  264:                 if (
  265:                     block5_renderer_distinct
  266:                     < min(
  267:                         4,
  268:                         len(block5_paths),
  269:                     )
  270:                 ):
  271:                     raise RuntimeError(
  272:                         "Stage2B renderer visual QA rejected "
  273:                         "insufficient distinct imagery."
  274:                     )
  275: 
  276:                 if block5_renderer_max_run > 2:
  277:                     raise RuntimeError(
  278:                         "Stage2B renderer visual QA rejected "
  279:                         "more than 2 consecutive identical "
  280:                         "beat images."
  281:                     )
  282: 
  283:                 if (
  284:                     len(block5_paths) >= 8
  285:                     and block5_renderer_share > 0.42
  286:                 ):
  287:                     raise RuntimeError(
  288:                         "Stage2B renderer visual QA rejected "
  289:                         "a dominant repeated beat image."
  290:                     )
  291: 
  292:                 # BLOCK5_RENDERER_QA_END
  293: 
  294:                 raw_durations = []
  295: 
  296:                 for beat in visual_beats:
  297: 
  298:                     try:
  299:                         duration = float(
  300:                             beat.get(
  301:                                 "target_duration",
  302:                                 2.5,
  303:                             )
  304:                         )
  305:                     except (
  306:                         TypeError,
  307:                         ValueError,
  308:                     ):
  309:                         duration = 2.5
  310: 
  311:                     # Hook-biased pacing:
  312:                     # first visual changes are deliberately
  313:                     # quicker; later explanation/payoff beats
  314:                     # are allowed slightly more breathing room.
  315:                     beat_position = len(
  316:                         raw_durations
  317:                     )
  318: 
  319:                     if beat_position == 0:
  320:                         pace_multiplier = 0.62
  321:                     elif beat_position == 1:
  322:                         pace_multiplier = 0.72
  323:                     elif beat_position == 2:
  324:                         pace_multiplier = 0.82
  325:                     elif beat_position >= max(
  326:                         0,
  327:                         len(visual_beats) - 2,
  328:                     ):
  329:                         pace_multiplier = 1.12
  330:                     else:
  331:                         pace_multiplier = 1.0
  332: 
  333:                     duration *= pace_multiplier
  334: 
  335:                     raw_durations.append(
  336:                         max(
  337:                             0.65,
  338:                             min(
  339:                                 duration,
  340:                                 3.5,
  341:                             ),
  342:                         )
  343:                     )
  344: 
  345:                 raw_total = sum(
  346:                     raw_durations
  347:                 )
  348: 
  349:                 if raw_total <= 0:
  350:                     raw_total = float(
  351:                         len(raw_durations)
  352:                     )
  353: 
  354:                     raw_durations = [
  355:                         1.0
  356:                         for _ in raw_durations
  357:                     ]
  358: 
  359:                 scaled_durations = [
  360:                     audio_duration
  361:                     * duration
  362:                     / raw_total
  363:                     for duration in raw_durations
  364:                 ]
  365: 
  366:                 for beat_index, (
  367:                     beat,
  368:                     image,
  369:                     cut_duration,
  370:                 ) in enumerate(
  371:                     zip(
  372:                         visual_beats,
  373:                         beat_images,
  374:                         scaled_durations,
  375:                     ),
  376:                     start=1,
  377:                 ):
  378: 
  379:                     image_path = Path(
  380:                         image.image_path
  381:                     )
  382: 
  383:                     if not image_path.exists():
  384:                         raise FileNotFoundError(
  385:                             "Beat image not found: "
  386:                             f"{image_path}"
  387:                         )
  388: 
  389:                     # Controlled alternating reframes keep static
  390:                     # licensed imagery alive without fabricating
  391:                     # content or modifying its meaning.
  392:                     # Restrained motion pattern:
  393:                     # enough movement to prevent slideshow feel
  394:                     # without turning every shot into an effect.
  395:                     zoom_pattern = (
  396:                         1.00,
  397:                         1.025,
  398:                         1.050,
  399:                         1.015,
  400:                         1.040,
  401:                     )
  402: 
  403:                     zoom = zoom_pattern[
  404:                         (beat_index - 1)
  405:                         % len(zoom_pattern)
  406:                     ]
  407: 
  408:                     clip = self._cover_image_clip(
  409:                         image_path=image_path,
  410:                         duration=cut_duration,
  411:                         zoom=zoom,
  412:                     )
  413: 
  414:                     image_clips.append(
  415:                         clip
  416:                     )
  417: 
  418:             else:
  419: 
  420:                 # Stage 2A fallback.
  421:                 cut_allocation = {
  422:                     1: 4,
  423:                     2: 3,
  424:                     3: 3,
  425:                     4: 2,
  426:                 }
  427: 
  428:                 total_scene_duration = sum(
  429:                     max(
  430:                         float(scene.duration),
  431:                         0.01,
  432:                     )
  433:                     for scene in scenes
  434:                 )
  435: 
  436:                 for scene_index, (
  437:                     scene,
  438:                     image,
  439:                 ) in enumerate(
  440:                     zip(
  441:                         scenes,
  442:                         images,
  443:                     ),
  444:                     start=1,
  445:                 ):
  446: 
  447:                     image_path = Path(
  448:                         image.image_path
  449:                     )
  450: 
  451:                     if not image_path.exists():
  452:                         raise FileNotFoundError(
  453:                             f"Image not found: {image_path}"
  454:                         )
  455: 
  456:                     cuts = cut_allocation.get(
  457:                         scene_index,
  458:                         2,
  459:                     )
  460: 
  461:                     scene_share = (
  462:                         max(
  463:                             float(scene.duration),
  464:                             0.01,
  465:                         )
  466:                         / total_scene_duration
  467:                     )
  468: 
  469:                     stage_duration = (
  470:                         audio_duration
  471:                         * scene_share
  472:                     )
  473: 
  474:                     cut_duration = (
  475:                         stage_duration
  476:                         / cuts
  477:                     )
  478: 
  479:                     for local_cut in range(cuts):
  480: 
  481:                         zoom = (
  482:                             1.00
  483:                             if local_cut % 3 == 0
  484:                             else (
  485:                                 1.035
  486:                                 if local_cut % 3 == 1
  487:                                 else 1.065
  488:                             )
  489:                         )
  490: 
  491:                         clip = self._cover_image_clip(
  492:                             image_path=image_path,
  493:                             duration=cut_duration,
  494:                             zoom=zoom,
  495:                         )
  496: 
  497:                         image_clips.append(
  498:                             clip
  499:                         )
  500: 
  501:             ##################################################
  502:             # Combine micro-shot editorial timeline
  503:             ##################################################
  504: 
  505:             base_video = concatenate_videoclips(
  506:                 image_clips,
  507:                 method="compose",
  508:             )
  509: 
  510:             ##################################################
  511:             # Build speech-aligned subtitles
  512:             ##################################################
  513: 
  514:             try:
  515: 
  516:                 phrases = (
  517:                     await self.caption_aligner
  518:                     .align_phrases(
  519:                         str(audio_path)
  520:                     )
  521:                 )
  522: 
  523:                 for phrase in phrases:
  524: 
  525:                     subtitle = (
  526:                         self.subtitle_renderer
  527:                         .create_clip(
  528:                             text=phrase["text"],
  529:                             start_time=phrase[
  530:                                 "start_time"
  531:                             ],
  532:                             end_time=phrase[
  533:                                 "end_time"
  534:                             ],
  535:                         )
  536:                     )
  537: 
  538:                     subtitle_clips.append(
  539:                         subtitle
  540:                     )
  541: 
  542:             except Exception as exc:
  543: 
  544:                 caption_mode = "fallback"
  545: 
  546:                 print(
  547:                     "Whisper caption alignment "
  548:                     "failed. Using fallback "
  549:                     "captions."
  550:                 )
  551: 
  552:                 print(
  553:                     f"Alignment error: {exc}"
  554:                 )
  555: 
  556:                 ##################################################
  557:                 # Fallback proportional captions
  558:                 ##################################################
  559: 
  560:                 for scene in scenes:
  561: 
  562:                     phrase_clips = (
  563:                         self.subtitle_renderer
  564:                         .create_phrase_clips(
  565:                             text=scene.narration,
  566:                             start_time=scene.start_time,
  567:                             end_time=scene.end_time,
  568:                         )
  569:                     )
  570: 
  571:                     subtitle_clips.extend(
  572:                         phrase_clips
  573:                     )
  574: 
  575:             ##################################################
  576:             # Composite images + subtitles
  577:             ##################################################
  578: 
  579:             final_video = CompositeVideoClip(
  580:                 [
  581:                     base_video,
  582:                     *subtitle_clips,
  583:                 ],
  584:                 size=(
  585:                     self.WIDTH,
  586:                     self.HEIGHT,
  587:                 ),
  588:             )
  589: 
  590:             final_video = (
  591:                 final_video
  592:                 .with_audio(audio)
  593:                 .with_duration(
  594:                     audio_duration
  595:                 )
  596:             )
  597: 
  598:             ##################################################
  599:             # Safe filename
  600:             ##################################################
  601: 
  602:             safe_name = "".join(
  603:                 c if c.isalnum() else "_"
  604:                 for c in content.title
  605:             )[:60]
  606: 
  607:             output = (
  608:                 self.output_dir
  609:                 / f"{safe_name}.mp4"
  610:             )
  611: 
  612:             ##################################################
  613:             # Render
  614:             ##################################################
  615: 
  616:             final_video.write_videofile(
  617:                 str(output),
  618:                 fps=self.FPS,
  619:                 codec="libx264",
  620:                 audio_codec="aac",
  621:                 logger=None,
  622:             )
  623: 
  624:         finally:
  625: 
  626:             if final_video is not None:
  627:                 final_video.close()
  628: 
  629:             if base_video is not None:
  630:                 base_video.close()
  631: 
  632:             for subtitle in subtitle_clips:
  633:                 subtitle.close()
  634: 
  635:             for clip in image_clips:
  636:                 clip.close()
  637: 
  638:             audio.close()
  639: 
  640:         return {
  641:             "status": "success",
  642:             "video_path": str(output),
  643:             "title": content.title,
  644:             "duration": audio_duration,
  645:             "resolution": (
  646:                 f"{self.WIDTH}x{self.HEIGHT}"
  647:             ),
  648:             "fps": self.FPS,
  649:             "captions": True,
  650:             "caption_mode": caption_mode,
  651:             "visual_engine": (
  652:                 "stage2b_semantic_microbeat"
  653:                 if use_semantic_beats
  654:                 else "stage2a_microshot_fallback"
  655:             ),
  656:             "visual_cut_count": len(image_clips),
  657:             "story_stage_count": len(scenes),
  658:             "average_visual_cut_seconds": round(
  659:                 audio_duration / max(1, len(image_clips)),
  660:                 3,
  661:             ),
  662:             "hook_visual_strategy": (
  663:                 "accelerated_first_three_beats"
  664:                 if use_semantic_beats
  665:                 else "stage2a_fallback"
  666:             ),
  667:             "semantic_beat_count": (
  668:                 len(visual_beats)
  669:                 if visual_beats
  670:                 else 0
  671:             ),
  672:             "semantic_media_enabled": (
  673:                 use_semantic_beats
  674:             ),
  675:             "editorial_pacing": (
  676:                 "hook_biased_dynamic"
  677:                 if use_semantic_beats
  678:                 else "stage2a_fallback"
  679:             ),
  680:             "caption_phrase_target_words": 3,
  681:             "visual_framing": "aspect_preserving_cover",
  682:         }

   22: 
   23:     State persists across application restarts.
   24: 
   25:     Failed production is bounded per supervisor pass and repeated
   26:     failure enters a cooldown so external providers and local
   27:     generation services are not hammered continuously.
   28:     """
   29: 
   30:     def __init__(
   31:         self,
   32:         scheduler: ProductionScheduler,
   33:         *,
   34:         daily_target: int = 4,
   35:         state_path: str | Path = (
   36:             "generated/state/daily_batch_state.json"
   37:         ),
   38:         attempts_per_pass: int = 2,
   39:         failure_cooldown_seconds: int = 60 * 60,
   40:     ) -> None:
   41: 
   42:         if daily_target < 1:
   43:             raise ValueError(
   44:                 "daily_target must be at least 1."
   45:             )
   46: 
   47:         if attempts_per_pass < 1:
   48:             raise ValueError(
   49:                 "attempts_per_pass must be at least 1."
   50:             )
   51: 
   52:         if failure_cooldown_seconds < 0:
   53:             raise ValueError(
   54:                 "failure_cooldown_seconds cannot be negative."
   55:             )
   56: 
   57:         self.scheduler = scheduler
   58:         self.daily_target = int(daily_target)
   59:         self.state_path = Path(state_path)
   60: 
   61:         self.attempts_per_pass = int(
   62:             attempts_per_pass
   63:         )
   64: 
   65:         self.failure_cooldown_seconds = int(
   66:             failure_cooldown_seconds
   67:         )
   68: 
   69:         self.state_path.parent.mkdir(
   70:             parents=True,
   71:             exist_ok=True,
   72:         )
   73: 
   74:         self._batch_lock = asyncio.Lock()

   77:         self,
   78:     ) -> dict[str, Any]:
   79: 
   80:         async with self._batch_lock:
   81: 
   82:             if not self.scheduler.enabled:

--- DailyProductionBatch.run_daily_batch @ line 76 ---
   68: 
   69:         self.state_path.parent.mkdir(
   70:             parents=True,
   71:             exist_ok=True,
   72:         )
   73: 
   74:         self._batch_lock = asyncio.Lock()
   75: 
   76:     async def run_daily_batch(
   77:         self,
   78:     ) -> dict[str, Any]:
   79: 
   80:         async with self._batch_lock:
   81: 
   82:             if not self.scheduler.enabled:
   83:                 return {
   84:                     "status": "autonomy_disabled",
   85:                     "successful": 0,
   86:                     "daily_target": self.daily_target,
   87:                 }
   88: 
   89:             today = date.today().isoformat()
   90:             state = self._load_state()
   91: 
   92:             if state.get("date") != today:
   93: 
   94:                 state = {
   95:                     "date": today,
   96:                     "successful": 0,
   97:                     "attempted": 0,
   98:                     "results": [],
   99:                     "cooldown_until": None,
  100:                 }
  101: 
  102:                 self._save_state(state)
  103: 
  104:             successful = int(
  105:                 state.get("successful", 0)
  106:             )
  107: 
  108:             if successful >= self.daily_target:
  109: 
  110:                 return {
  111:                     "status": "daily_quota_complete",
  112:                     "date": today,
  113:                     "successful": successful,
  114:                     "daily_target": self.daily_target,
  115:                 }
  116: 
  117:             now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
  118: 
  119:             cooldown_until_raw = state.get(
  120:                 "cooldown_until"
  121:             )
  122: 
  123:             if cooldown_until_raw:
  124: 
  125:                 try:
  126:                     cooldown_until = (
  127:                         datetime.fromisoformat(
  128:                             str(cooldown_until_raw)
  129:                         )
  130:                     )
  131: 
  132:                     if cooldown_until.tzinfo is None:
  133:                         cooldown_until = (
  134:                             cooldown_until.replace(
  135:                                 tzinfo=timezone.utc
  136:                             )
  137:                         )
  138: 
  139:                     if now < cooldown_until:
  140: 
  141:                         return {
  142:                             "status": "cooldown",
  143:                             "date": today,
  144:                             "successful": successful,
  145:                             "daily_target": self.daily_target,
  146:                             "cooldown_until": (
  147:                                 cooldown_until.isoformat()
  148:                             ),
  149:                         }
  150: 
  151:                 except (TypeError, ValueError):
  152:                     logger.warning(
  153:                         "Invalid production cooldown state; "
  154:                         "clearing it."
  155:                     )
  156: 
  157:                 state["cooldown_until"] = None
  158: 
  159:             results = list(
  160:                 state.get("results", [])
  161:             )
  162: 
  163:             remaining = (
  164:                 self.daily_target - successful
  165:             )
  166: 
  167:             max_batch_attempts = min(
  168:                 remaining,
  169:                 self.attempts_per_pass,
  170:             )
  171: 
  172:             batch_attempts = 0
  173:             pass_successes = 0
  174: 
  175:             while (
  176:                 successful < self.daily_target
  177:                 and batch_attempts < max_batch_attempts
  178:             ):
  179: 
  180:                 batch_attempts += 1
  181: 
  182:                 logger.info(
  183:                     "Starting autonomous production attempt %s/%s "
  184:                     "for this supervisor pass. "
  185:                     "Daily progress: %s/%s.",
  186:                     batch_attempts,
  187:                     max_batch_attempts,
  188:                     successful,
  189:                     self.daily_target,
  190:                 )
  191: 
  192:                 result = await self.scheduler.run_once()
  193: 
  194:                 status = str(
  195:                     result.get(
  196:                         "status",
  197:                         "unknown",
  198:                     )
  199:                 )
  200: 
  201:                 record = {
  202:                     "timestamp": datetime.now(
  203:                         timezone.utc
  204:                     ).isoformat(),
  205:                     "status": status,
  206:                     "result": result,
  207:                 }
  208: 
  209:                 results.append(record)
  210: 
  211:                 state["attempted"] = (
  212:                     int(
  213:                         state.get(
  214:                             "attempted",
  215:                             0,
  216:                         )
  217:                     )
  218:                     + 1
  219:                 )
  220: 
  221:                 if status == "success":
  222: 
  223:                     successful += 1
  224:                     pass_successes += 1
  225: 
  226:                     state["successful"] = successful
  227: 
  228:                     # A success proves the pipeline is making
  229:                     # forward progress. Clear previous cooldown.
  230:                     state["cooldown_until"] = None
  231: 
  232:                 state["results"] = results
  233:                 self._save_state(state)
  234: 
  235:                 logger.info(
  236:                     "Jarvis daily production progress: %s/%s.",
  237:                     successful,
  238:                     self.daily_target,
  239:                 )
  240: 
  241:             completed = (
  242:                 successful >= self.daily_target
  243:             )
  244: 
  245:             # If this entire supervisor pass failed to produce
  246:             # anything, wait before trying another batch.
  247:             if (
  248:                 not completed
  249:                 and batch_attempts > 0
  250:                 and pass_successes == 0
  251:                 and self.failure_cooldown_seconds > 0
  252:             ):
  253: 
  254:                 from datetime import timedelta
  255: 
  256:                 cooldown_dt = (
  257:                     datetime.now(timezone.utc)
  258:                     + timedelta(
  259:                         seconds=self.failure_cooldown_seconds
  260:                     )
  261:                 )
  262: 
  263:                 state["cooldown_until"] = (
  264:                     cooldown_dt.isoformat()
  265:                 )
  266: 
  267:                 self._save_state(state)
  268:

   88:             return False
   89: 
   90:         task = self._task
   91:         self._task = None
   92: 
   93:         if task.done():
   94:             try:
   95:                 task.result()
   96:             except asyncio.CancelledError:
   97:                 pass
   98:             except Exception:
   99:                 logger.exception(
  100:                     "Production scheduler task failed."
  101:                 )
  102: 
  103:             return True
  104: 
  105:         task.cancel()
  106: 
  107:         try:
  108:             await task
  109:         except asyncio.CancelledError:
  110:             pass
  111: 
  112:         return True

  144:     async def run_once(self) -> dict:
  145:         """Attempt one autonomous production cycle with safe retries."""
  146: 
  147:         attempt = 1
  148: 
  149:         while True:
  150:             try:
  151:                 result = await self.runner.run_cycle()
  152: 
  153:                 failure = result.get(
  154:                     "failure"
  155:                 )
  156: 
  157:                 result_retryable = (
  158:                     isinstance(failure, dict)
  159:                     and failure.get("retryable") is True
  160:                 )
  161: 
  162:                 if (
  163:                     result_retryable
  164:                     and attempt
  165:                     < self.retry_policy.max_attempts
  166:                 ):
  167:                     delay = (
  168:                         self.retry_policy.delay_for_retry(
  169:                             attempt
  170:                         )
  171:                     )
  172: 
  173:                     logger.warning(
  174:                         "Retryable production result on "
  175:                         "attempt %s/%s. Retrying in %.2fs. "
  176:                         "Category: %s",
  177:                         attempt,
  178:                         self.retry_policy.max_attempts,
  179:                         delay,
  180:                         failure.get(
  181:                             "category",
  182:                             "unknown",
  183:                         ),
  184:                     )
  185: 
  186:                     await asyncio.sleep(
  187:                         delay
  188:                     )
  189: 
  190:                     attempt += 1
  191:                     continue
  192: 
  193:                 return result
  194: 
  195:             except ProductionCycleBusyError:
  196:                 return {
  197:                     "status": "skipped",
  198:                     "reason": "production_busy",
  199:                     "attempts": attempt,
  200:                 }
  201: 
  202:             except asyncio.CancelledError:
  203:                 raise
  204: 
  205:             except Exception as exc:
  206:                 should_retry = (
  207:                     self.retry_policy.is_retryable_exception(
  208:                         exc
  209:                     )
  210:                     and attempt
  211:                     < self.retry_policy.max_attempts
  212:                 )
  213: 
  214:                 if not should_retry:
  215:                     logger.exception(
  216:                         "Scheduled production cycle failed."
  217:                     )
  218: 
  219:                     return {
  220:                         "status": "scheduler_failed",
  221:                         "reason": "production_cycle_failed",
  222:                         "attempts": attempt,
  223:                         "error_type": type(exc).__name__,
  224:                     }
  225: 
  226:                 delay = (
  227:                     self.retry_policy.delay_for_retry(
  228:                         attempt
  229:                     )
  230:                 )
  231: 
  232:                 logger.warning(
  233:                     "Transient production failure on "
  234:                     "attempt %s/%s. Retrying in %.2fs.",
  235:                     attempt,
  236:                     self.retry_policy.max_attempts,
  237:                     delay,
  238:                 )
  239: 
  240:                 await asyncio.sleep(
  241:                     delay
  242:                 )
  243: 
  244:                 attempt += 1
  245: 
  246:     async def _run_loop(self) -> None:
  247:         """Run scheduled cycles until cancelled."""
  248: 
  249:         while True:
  250:             await asyncio.sleep(
  251:                 self.interval_seconds
  252:             )

--- ProductionScheduler._run_loop @ line 246 ---
  238:                 )
  239: 
  240:                 await asyncio.sleep(
  241:                     delay
  242:                 )
  243: 
  244:                 attempt += 1

  246:     async def _run_loop(self) -> None:
  247:         """Run scheduled cycles until cancelled."""
  248: 
  249:         while True:
  250:             await asyncio.sleep(
  251:                 self.interval_seconds
  252:             )
  253: 
  254:             try:
  255:                 await self.run_once()

  262:                     "Unexpected scheduler-loop failure."
